The reformation error String in FK `formation_check.rs evaluate` (snapshot key `structural/formation_check.rs:910`, root `structural/formation_check.rs:864:evaluate`) is bounded at one allocation per `evaluate` call: `map_err` runs on Err and the immediate `?` returns it, with no catch, retry or continuation in the guarded function. The full evaluate multiplicity is kept: the graph must bind `check_at`'s sole `evaluate` edge (parent `structural/formation_check.rs:455:check_at`). Its 8334 B content and capacity 2 remain; no visit, q traversal, graph/function call count, source/manifest owner, foreign/preclosure failure or downstream detail/unavailable/Preview/W2 copy is removed. KD5C6's positive/post-rebound and override-alias refusal semantics are reused for this one site, with an exact separate marker, source/rule, qualified caller/graph, inventory and pre/post witness. Every unknown source, changed rule, conflicting override or erased current owner stops pricing.

**Shared illustrative layouts** (`g3lib.py`). FormationRow is carried at the existing helper estimate 3·s(ExactAccumulator) + 128 (3,440 B in the illustrative 64-bit table) and `(usize,[f64;4])` at 5·s(usize) (40 B). The ordinary constructor and the final G4 evaluator run in separate processes, so a helper-local ASSUMED update cannot populate the final evaluator; declaring these two values in `g3lib` makes every process use the same illustrative inventory. Symbolic coefficients and the actual G5 in-build bindings are unchanged: FormationRow and the tuple require their own native observation before qualification, and missing or unknown atoms still stop evaluation.

### 8.3 Exclusive allocation choices and owned first errors

**Row-source ownership** (`b7a_owner_choices`, FK `structural.rs row_sources`). The audited success and the terminal Range fallback are exclusive per DOF, and partitioned force terms are cloned at most q per full audit, so the `site_from` is q = 12·l per call of `audit_load_fidelity` and `audit_sparse_load_fidelity`; it replaces §8.2's exact 24·l preimage. Both caller counts, the 128-byte String class and capacity 2 remain.

**Metadata base recipes** (`retained_product.rs validate_final_metadata`). Metadata validation selects one complete expected-base-id String from ten mutually exclusive recipe constructors, including the exclusive support combination/case alternatives. Their source-qualified content bounds are 46, 37, 140, 143, 158, 162, 188, 338, 169 and 322 B (`BOUNDS`); the largest complete base is 338 B. The allocation volume uses one capacity-2 generation per full validator call, without changing its physical visits or any numerical, row or scan work. The report keeps every potential site's standalone bound and names the nonnegative allocation group that replaces their mutually exclusive sum. The prematch owned `stable_suffix`, displacement/stress signs, postmatch combination wrapper, qualified-case helper and their owned suffixes remain independent positive generations. Errors, RefusedCase and receipt failures, `public_error`/`capture_error` detail copies, STAGED/failed and JSON owners also remain. The exact current source/cap/custody/helper/enum, full caller/stack and allocation inventories, authoritative audit preimages and competing override checks bind this local group; no global STATIC, RID, error or default class changes. The envelope-only exclusion is restricted to the existing canonical emitter.

**Metadata first errors** (`ERROR_SITES` in `bind_rows_subject`'s path). The three metadata errors keep their full 149, 1,049 and 1,046-byte content and capacity 2. Each Err propagates immediately through `?` out of the whole `bind_rows_subject` invocation, so each site allocates at most once per full owner call. All freeze, serializer and combination callers remain, including later failures beside other Frozen cases. Association/detail/JSON/RefusedCase and receipt failures keep their independent owned generations and copies. This is a positive per-invocation bound, never a prepared-custody zero.

**Five owned first errors** (`b7a_returning_errors`, marker `b7a_owned_first_errors`). Five owned error allocations are bounded at one allocation per full enclosing function invocation: two format sites in `lib.rs exact_straight_summary_extrema`, one in `recover_curved_bend_local_forces`, and one each in `retained_product.rs check_support_maps` and `support_observables`. The first three immediately propagate through the outer Result with `?`; the latter two directly return their owned Association String. The five allocation kinds, full caller inventories, source/type bodies, rule aliases and pre/post counts are checked before pricing and around the format/lexical `SITE_FROM` override. The common rules reach both routes without replacing route E's entries. Existing content/capacity bounds, numeric and scan work, successful equivalents and every downstream detail, display, clone and capture owner remain positive. The rule uses count-only precision; it does not use the optional typed 55-byte span bound. Full raw `exact_sum.rs` and `curved_bend` bindings keep the reviewed receiver/error closure.

**Envelope-only projection** (`ENVZERO`). The envelope-only diagnostic projection keeps its exact `ENVZERO` source declaration, run helper and `tb` emitter (pinned in `MODE_SOURCE`). Only its two excluded retained_product allocation sites have zero counts in this mode, and their zero multiplicity and absent owner counts are required. The three lib.rs sites keep positive witnesses there, and all five keep positive witnesses in base/X/W. Unknown, partial or extra exclusions, changed emitter code and arbitrary default zeros refuse; every source/rule/caller/site inventory check still runs.

### 8.4 Scoped TEXT (`b7a_scoped_text_model`)

Current producer spellings are bounded at their exact sites (`LN` keys), with no global message or error class change. Each replacement checks its authoritative audit preimage first.
- **`pressure_runtime.rs problem`.** The private pressure problem keeps all calls and owned copies: message 453 B, five refs with 760 collection bytes, 644 joined bytes and 718 complete id bytes. Its converted ref loop (`iter:refs.iter().map`, 5 per call) is qualified to this owner; the global four-ref rule and every other helper call remain. `problem()` keeps its nine callers (`PROBLEM_PARENTS`). B7b's `validate_profile` messages are at most 327 B with at most two refs, inside the 453/760 bounds.

- **Unit message.** The source-local unit message (lib.rs) is 600 B; the authoritative audit and the lexical rule change together, and its 702-byte format is unchanged.
- **Reader `require`.** physics_source.rs `require` keeps all its call sites (96 in the current source) and both formatted detail temporaries, with 40 detail bytes and 15 prefix bytes. Of the 96 calls, 94 pass a literal detail, the longest 37 B (`ACTUAL_MATERIAL_DUPLICATE_TEMPERATURE`); the two formatted details are `ACTUAL_PAIR_INPUT_RANGE:{stage}` and `ACTUAL_DERIVED_SHEAR_RANGE:{stage}`.
- **Literals.** Mode, sign and arc clones keep all calls and positive 46/167/47-byte literal storage.

The individual reason, fired, joined and sentence maxima are unchanged, and the same aggregate H reaches every independent finding, capture, JSON, ordinary body and staged copy. No formation site or owner is removed.
- **Admitted ref slots.** Admitted copied envelope and receipt ref slots stay governed by the components-empty domain: the only five-ref calls require an arc, and the sole arc builder consumes the excluded components. Raw, shared, refused and headless five-ref conversions and spellings remain positive.
- **Guard.** The units source (`UNITS_SHA`), the range-origin inventory and the inherited formation pins bind these spellings.

### 8.5 Route and phase model (`b7a_phase_model`)

**Counts** (`COUNTS`, `COUNT_ROLES`). The model separates actual authored cases (lc) from the typed storage ceiling (C_eq, caps key c), the combination slots z and the per-case inputs l. On admitted route E the components, element and named-basis refusals and the empty pressure regions bind plain nodal force terms (q ≤ l per case), one base state and no formed primitive, manifest, expansion or load-row finding producer: force l, formed 0, named 0, states 1, findings 0. Route L has force and formed 12·l per case, C_eq named states (1 + C_eq states) and C_eq ordinary findings. Generic force, closure-record/Option, vector, wrapper/null, reader, raw census and unrelated ordinary owners stay positive. Refused or foreign ordinary calls do not use this admitted pricing warrant. The effective TEXT force/source q bounds stay conservative at 12·l on both routes.

**Successful publication context** (W1 stage 2). Stage 2 retires the stage-1 preparation-refusal premise (`INVALIDATION`): a successful route-L case may contain member loads, so the successful formed-load and finding count is the full authored lc, including at (lc, z) = (1, 2); it is never max(lc − 1, 0). Full ordinary/fallback ownership remains. A separate successful-publication context (`run_success_branch`) keeps all actual ordinary calls and authored lc·l inputs, and runs the canonical base/env/X/W consumers and the downstream cost chain independently. Only six closed formed-only factories (`formed_owner`: assembled children, typed source children, scaled-force children, recovery-source children, uniform constructor and element application) and the isolated finding children use this occurrence count. Each factory rounds every nonnegative integer atom coefficient upward before the existing C_eq multiplication, so C_eq·result ≥ owners·value for every atom. Moving/peak owners, common nodal work, generic ORDINARY and formation parent wrappers, named L states and all other storage/copy charges stay at their full bounds. The failed-manifest branch (one Preview/base/nodal case on route L; `FAILED`) and the simultaneous original/manifest/staged and PreparedCases holdings keep their conservative positive charges.

**Phase selection** (`merge_success_tree`). Only W3, W4 and W5 select the success context's forms; W1, W2 and X1–X3 keep the full ordinary/fallback forms. Both native modes and all sixteen phase identities (fourteen before §8.14) and report fields are preserved, as are the coefficient-wise route union and the shared native bindings and atom values. Full ordinary TAV_W/TAV_X forms and text atoms remain unchanged, so the runtime law and the evaluator keep the full upward 5 % TEXT reserve. No illustrative ASSUMED value is replaced with a success scalar. **Guard:** the branch's policy, text binder, illustrative atoms, spelling maxima and phase inventory must equal the ordinary run's.

**Formation formats** (`TOTALS`, `FORMAT_ROWS` at `formation_guard.rs` 239, 241, 251 and 322). The four formation formats use only their guarded row/finding generations. Exact dual markers, source/admission bindings, the qualified caller and loop stacks (`solve_prepared_load_case → load_row_finding → decide_row`), the format/lexical inventory and the canonical total preimages guard them. Competing zero, from, size, aggregate or total aliases, an erased or changed M(`load_row_finding`) = lc, and incorrect effective pre/post row counts refuse. The capacity factor 2 is independent of the loop iteration count, and the individual reason/fired/join/sentence maxima and all full capture/JSON copies remain.

### 8.6 W1 reductions (`w1_reductions_model`)

`w1_reductions_model.py` pins the RF and the reduced producer/consumer cohorts explicitly (`RF_PINS`, `REDUCED_PINS`; `FormationSource.loads` selects the cohort). A half change or a changed lifetime stops, and marker absence never silently selects historical prices. The reductions establish source and ownership warrants, not runtime allocation observations; stage-2 member additions do not change the reserved nodal layouts.
- **L1a** (W1-L1a). The reduced cohort borrows typed primitives: `FormationSource.loads` is a `Cow<'a, [LoadPrimitive]>`, so the typed source children and the recovery-source primitive copy are not separate owners.
- **L1b.** The typed formation keeps one closure-comparison transient (one manifest copy) instead of q.
- **L1c** (W1-L1c). Scaled-force children are charged only in their call-local nonzero-scale evaluation: with b = 0 the evaluation borrows f, and the sole scaled force drops inside the second evaluation, before the caller tail. The existing header allowance is kept.
- **L2.** Uniform-constructor and attachment staging is bounded by one sequential call and one attach-last term; moved returned rows stay separately resident.
- **L3** (W1-L3). Formation Values move into the receipt body, whose positive owner already includes them; there is no separate `json!(&Value)` transient tree (`FORMATION_VALUE_BUILD` is empty on the reduced cohort).
- **L5** (W1-L5) guards the combination index and its callee by `combining` (§4.7, with U6-RB's shared guard). Route L at z ≥ 1 separately prices the callee's sorted `base_ids` and cloned `case_rows`.

Source/lifetime mutation tests and the actual driver exit-code controls are in `tests/test_w1_reductions.py`.

### 8.7 W1 stage-2 owners (`w1_member_load_model`)

The complete source cohort (`SOURCE_FILES`) binds member-only custody, the once-formed exact ledger, recovery/conditioning, both lanes, the loaded maxima and the independent reader call chains. The model's constants are source capacity bounds, never measured RSS or allocator usable bytes. All native strides come from the generated build bindings. Ordinary and fallback owners remain. **Guard:** a changed pinned file, the return of the retired preparation refusal, or changed member-only invocation custody stops the run.

| Rule | Actual source owners and bound |
|---|---|
| **W1-MC** (code comment `W1-CAP/LED/REC/L4` in `producer_caps.py`; `producer_additions` T11.12, T12.11, T12.12, T13.4) | `ProductCapture.member_sources` owns one optional sidecar vector. Each member case owns its copied case id, `MemberTermIdentity` vector and separate `MemberLoadSet`; both term and load ids are charged. Validation sort/used bitmaps, prepared/rebuilt sidecar clones and the submitted borrowed `MemberSourceRef` vector remain positive in their own rows. No vector is added to `SourceParts`, `PrimitiveSource`, `CaseSlot` or `EarlyCaseSource`. |
| **W1-MID** | `copy_member_id` has the exact original `copy` allocation, requested-byte, reserve, push, capacity, event and error body. Its three productive callers copy the two captured load ids and one prepared local-load id. Capture binds each normalized id to its authored occurrence, and later preparation binds the same captured member identity; D1's raw/typed census and enforcement bound every such id to 128 bytes. Only this helper's `push_str` site takes the positive 128 B class. All calls, the TEXT capacity factor two, typed String/ID owners and moving storage remain; generic `copy` and nodal/diagnostic callers keep their previous classes. Full source pins, exact body/caller/test-shadow checks and D1 enforcement pins refuse drift. |
| **W1-MP** | The member occurrence predicate directly matches `(global_x,0)`, `(global_y,1)` or `(global_z,2)`. D1 already requires exactly those member direction strings, and the normalized axis is checked below three before matching. The member-loop parser call is removed in source; no call-graph zero hides it. The nodal occurrence loop keeps its original `parse_direction` call and the global parser is unchanged. Full source pins and an explicit predicate/nodal-parser inventory bind this specialization. |
| **W1-ML** (code comments `W1-SRC/LED` for `E_src`/`E_led` and `W1-CAP/LED/REC/L4`; T12.13) | `CasePrep` keeps its original nodal ledger beside the once-formed ledger. The latter therefore pays all declared six-entry records and every one of N = 6n possible net DOFs, each with up to 128 limbs; it takes no N − v credit against the old ledger. DOF discovery's requested/moving vectors are separate. The source encoding adds its conditional four-byte count and 32 + id bytes per member entry. The complete ledger encoding uses all N rows. Old/new encoding overlap during construction stays positive. |
| **W1-MR** (code comment `W1-CAP/LED/REC/L4`; T13.5, T14.4) | Recovery's local-load vector and E's scale vector pay their combined twelve Wide values and two tags per member, their headers and separate local arithmetic staging. Both lanes independently retain their new curvature vectors. Native `MemberOperators` includes the actual Wide length field at each width; the profile binds the actual resource atom rather than an eight-byte length estimate. |
| **W1-MM** (code comment `W1-CAP/LED/REC/L4`; T14.5) | One loaded-member search owns at most 256 `SearchNode` records of 2,744 B, 702,464 B total. Its helper/caller endpoint arrays, arithmetic buffers, moves and auxiliary records are conservatively bounded together below the separately charged 1 MiB search cap (`loaded_inventory`). PP's member-local input/id copies and returned three-axis spans, the previous PP maximum helper, returned hulls, completion work, evidence and boxed exhaustion diagnostic remain independent positive owners. Searches are sequential across members and lanes; exhaustion does not enlarge the live-node backing. |
| **W1-G8** (form `W1_MEMBER_READER`, every phase) | Every phase adds 2·C·1 MiB, 6,291,456 B at C = 3, for the complete new reader call chain and equal moving allowance. The original `G8_VALUES` and `G8_BYTES` owners remain. Independent recomputation keeps all 128 declarations; each stored normalized entry has at most 1024 bits, and a conservative 32-limb capacity pays carry/normalization staging. An additional 64 complete 128-limb integers cover declaration locals, nested Nat/Dyad operands/results, net/hash staging and old/new allocations. Expected-term records are borrowed fixed records. In the actual G8 call chain, the invocation guard bounds ids to 128 bytes and authored terms jointly to 128; exact nodal/member occurrence binding precedes native encoding/hash. A conservative encoded bound is 56,554 B, even allowing 192 references per support. General Vec growth gives final plus prior capacity below 3·56,554 = 169,662 B; no power-of-two assumption is made. Adding 32,768 B auxiliary staging and 131,072 B stack gives 333,502 B for encoding. Recomputation's 457,728 B bound dominates. Encoding/hash and recomputation have separate scopes. The standalone helper's looser 1024-byte/separate-count guards and its allocation controls are distinct from this production call-chain warrant. Native layouts dominate the independently checked wasm pairs. Requested-Layout and native/real-wasm stack controls exercise the proof; an observed peak is not its source bound. **Guard:** the run stops if the reader or loaded-search inventory exceeds its 1 MiB cap. |
| **W1-DISC** (`g4_caps.py`) | The selected member diagnostic's fixed text (`SELECTED_MEMBER_LOAD_MESSAGE`, read at the snapshot) exceeds the original 300-byte selected-message allowance, so the successor's diagnostic strings add its excess per attempted case; the original diagnostic owner is kept. |
| **W1-WIRE** (`g4_caps.py`) | The receipt source adds the optional nonempty member terms, one record of four slots and six entries per authored load per attempted case, and the optional `member_load_terms` parent key. |
| **W1-DIAG** (`g4_caps.py`) | The proof trace adds one optional exhaustion diagnostic with two endpoints of 16 fixed hex limbs, with its full positive grammar retained even on successful cases, and the curvature-capacity pair once on each lane; the nodal path still serializes only its original nine capacity entries. |
| **W1-GAP** (`O10_EDGE_ZERO` `retained_product.rs prepared_maxima` → NI `nonlinear_supports gap`) | The source-pinned `prepared_maxima` binds `gap` as an f64 tuple member and passes it to its number closure; the by-name value scan reads it as the nonlinear support constructor, which is never called. Only this impossible target is removed; real nonlinear constructors remain. |

The member path's exact new headers (`text_decisions`) are bounded by l, the three intensity components and the 16 diagnostic/proof limbs, each confined to its function, with unchanged identifier laws (`source` STATIC 128 at the two prepared-source clones; the generated self-weight id's case and pipe ids IN128 and their lengths NUM). W1 extracts the submitted-source loop into `solve_sources_projected`: the complete historical source-clone audit preimage (`retained/adaptive.rs:5082`) is re-installed at the actual clone statement after line carry, and the existing `sources.iter()` rule still bounds the moved loop by att; no owner or visit is deleted. The complete owner/call-chain pins and mutation controls are in `base/w1_member_load_model.py` and `tests/test_w1_member_load_model.py`; design emulation is not allocation evidence.

### 8.8 T4-U4b/U5b actual-source D3 (shared epoch 4)

Epoch 4 (model 0.6.0, the `-2` base pair, T4-U4b's derived stress rows, T4-U5b's tie rods and contribution groups, and the W1 not-covered passthrough) re-prices by D3. Source pins moved only after the epoch diff of each pinned file was read; the pin comments in each `base/*_model.py` say what changed. The line map re-keyed every carried rule; `linemap.pruned` held the same 31 rules as the registered tree's run, differing only in carried line numbers. Rules added for the epoch, by label:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **U4B-B** (`o10_rules.py`) | The epoch renames the two bases to `pressure-2` and `preview-physics-2`. Routes, projections and dispatch are B7b's, so B7b's route-E rules apply to the `-2` pair; pressure-source-1 stays excluded. |
| **U4B-RL** (`sq2_decisions_L.py`, typed patches) | preview-physics-2 publishes on each straight member the 51 preview-physics-1 rows plus σ⁺, σ⁻ and τ_v,max at five stations on one section (15 rows): 66. The producer states the same count for the failed-ordinary manifest (`retained_product.rs FAILED_MEMBER_ROWS`, read at the snapshot); a member with ν out of domain publishes 5 fewer, so 66 bounds every member. Route L's P_final = 7n + 66m + 8g + 3 and R0 = 7n + 66m + g + 3. **Guard:** the constant must be 51 or 66. |
| **U4B-RP** (`sq2_decisions_E.py`) | An exact member adds σ⁺, σ⁻ and τ_v,max at five stations on both bases (30 rows) to D-5's 72: 102. The census (`data/census_u4b_7c77bf8072.json`, `census_exact.py` on the pressure-source-1 fixtures as regenerated by their writer) measures 7n + 102m + 8g rows on every empty-region exact case; its evidence facts equal D-5's. **Guard:** the census must state 51 + 21 + 30. |
| **U4B-K** | A realized arc's certified maximum (or its unavailable warning) uses the straight maximum's id template with the basis segment (`_RID_D5`); the headline statement and the unavailable list push are counted once more per basis. Arcs are absent on D1 (P2), but the sites stay charged. |
| **U4B-L, U5B-L** (loops) | U4b's station loops are bounded by the five stations and two bases; the not-covered passes by m and by the case's rows (P). Every tie-rod loop is empty under D1: a rod exists only in `ObjectiveConnector::with_tie_rods`, called only by `joint::build_connector`, and P3 keeps connectors empty. |
| **U4B-ID** (identifier audits) | Each new text argument is an admitted id (≤ 128 B, or the case-qualified result id class), a static literal, a station token or a fixed hyphen segment; `final_row_id`, `pressure_row_id`, `shear_sign` and the Poisson diagnostic take their existing id classes. |
| **U4B-RD** (`SITE_FROM`, reader failures) | `Route::fail` allocates only when it returns `Err`, and every check in `u4b_evidence.rs` propagates with `?` to its reader entry (`validate_physics_evidence_in`, `validate_preview_physics_evidence`, `gates_after_g0`, `reader_verdict`): at most one failure text per entry call. |
| **U4B-NQ** | `rows.rs bind_for`'s `derived_not_qualified` loop is bounded by the case's rows; each treatment names at most three input ids. |
| **U4B-J3** (test code) | The census's capability test formats two report lines at J3's multiplicity 0; its `largest.max(..)` receivers are `u64`, so no edge reaches `FallbackError::max`. |
| **U5B-TR** | `load_closure`'s TieRodReference arm adds one `Failure::Closure` refusal inside the existing 8,352 B Closure class; `formation_check.rs`'s binding refusal formats the formed term's source (283 B). Connectors only (P3). |
| **U5B-JN, U5B-REC** (call graph) | The tie rod's direction and length go through the crate's `norm3`; `ObjectiveConnector::force_scaled` maps each rod through `TieRod::force_scaled`, not itself. |
| **U5B-JV** (`O10_EDGE_ZERO`) | `joint.rs hardware`'s `Err(Some(problem)) => problems.push(problem)` passes a String binding; the by-name scan picked `pressure_runtime`'s private `fn problem`, which `joint.rs` never calls. Only that edge is zeroed. |
| **U5B-ID, U5B-JT** | `TieRodError`'s name is one static literal. Joint hardware findings decode only `model.components` (empty on D1, P2); a joined list is bounded by D1's raw census, at most 16,384 values of one 256 B problem each plus all 131,072 authored string bytes. |

**Positive owners** (`base/u4b_u5b_owners_model.py`, hooked into `ordinary_caps.py`, `producer_caps.py`, `g4_caps.py` and `t25_g4.py`). Each owner is bound to the sha256 of the function bodies that allocate it; a snapshot without them adds nothing, and a changed body stops the run. No zero and no reuse credit is taken, and every owner is charged at push capacity with the previous backing of its last reallocation. Native strides come from new in-build atoms in `g5_profile.py`.
- **U5B-PD.** `published_nodal_displacements` collects N corrected values with size-hint lower bound 0: 8·pushcap(N) plus the previous backing, per case, in the ordinary recovery phase.
- **U4B-ST.** One member's station quantities: the bases and their clone, the shear factors, the normal and directional witness lists (≤ 5 station pairs each) and one station's extremes.
- **U5B-AB.** `ElementAbsorption`'s `vec![false; n]` (N bytes) in each of the three contribution-sum calls (contribution, load-fidelity and intended-action audits).
- **U4B-W1.** `prepared_not_covered`'s operand table (m × `[[Option<usize>; 5]; 5]`, per attempted case, T14), and `failed_manifest`'s Poisson flags (m × `Option<f64>`) with its diagnostics clone grown by ≤ m warnings, per requested case. `ordinary.diagnostics.clone()` has exactly D_env slots (the staged copy's own capacity), so its first push reallocates to 2·D_env (Rust's amortized growth, `grown_from`); the grown backing is charged beside the staged copy. T4-S6 corrected the earlier pushcap(D_env + m) plus half of it, which is below 2·D_env whenever D_env + m stays under a power of two (route E: 49,152 against 57,598 slots, +3,648,672 B in every phase).
- **U4B-RD.** In T17 V5 (G7), the base read's and route L's model checks' row `Index` (a growing hash table over the envelope rows, once per reader), the Poisson exemption sets, the station covers (route E: every case, member and basis), the material and pressurized-arc indices, and two copies of the captured model for `preview_model_checks`' per-lookup array clones.
- **U4B-NQ.** `not_qualified_for`'s vector of ≤ 30m treatments with its previous backing (T25.3); their strings move into the ledger, which already pays them. Its two filtered loops (`not_qualified_for`'s pass over the case's rows and `bind_for`'s pass over its treatments) are bounded by 30m, not P_final (T4-S6): the filter admits only the U4b derived kinds, which only `u4b_rows` pushes (append_station_quantities and the failed-manifest placeholders, bodies pinned), at most σ⁺, σ⁻ and τ_v,max at five stations on two bases per member, with no realized arc (P2). **Guard:** each kind's literal occurs once in PP production code and only `u4b_rows.rs` passes the kinds to a row constructor (four sites); otherwise the run stops, and without the pinned bodies the loops keep P.
- **U5B-CG** (T4-S6). Each AssemblyEvidence (dense and sparse) owns `contribution_groups`, a `Vec<usize>` push-built with one entry per frame or curved element and per ground spring (`structural_adapter.rs`; curved elements and connectors are absent on D1, P2 and P3): 8·pushcap(m + s) per evidence owner, charged in the typed core's evidence term, so three times per case with W2's two evaluations (4,608 B in O_base at the caps). **Guard:** the four allocating lines occur once and the move into the evidence twice.
- **Failed Preview formulation** (T4-S6; `b7a_domain_model.formulation_content`). The failed Preview manifest's formulation text is read from the current literals as the larger of its two forms: `preview_physics::formulation_basis` (its profile id, the seven `LIMITATIONS` and two recovery sentences: 9 limitation strings, 3,138 B) and `preview_formulation_basis` with `formulation_basis_for_model_with_source`'s two appended sentences (2,288 B). Raw literal bytes bound the decoded text. This replaces the B7a basis's 2,035 B in the held failed-manifest envelope owner (+3,309 B in O_base at three cases) and raises the `retained_product.rs` formulation-content lexical class from 2,914 B to the same 3,138 B (+896 B of TEXT). A changed constructor structure stops the run.

**S6-HS** (T4-S6; `u6_owners_model.domain_hash_streams`, `receipt_hash_streams`, `reader_hash_streams`, `stream_route`; priced in `g4_caps.py` and `t25_g4.py`). The three publication-hash families now stream: `retained_wire.rs domain_hash` (T16), result_export `source_blocks::domain_hash` (T17's G1, G3 and G8 hashes) and `source_receipt.rs hash` (every T25 route) feed the checked canonical text of the borrowed `{domain, payload}` to SHA-256 through `open_pipe_stress_canonical_json::checked_v1_domain_canonical` with a `ByteSink`. `retained_precision.rs` G1 (and `validate_publication_hash`) hash the publication as the source without its receipt (`DomainPayload::ObjectWithout`), so no public clone is made. The writer reproduces the former path's text and refusals exactly (the crate's `checked_v1_domain_canonical` documentation; byte-identity tests over every committed JSON document and the boundary cases). A streamed hash holds, beside the borrowed payload: the per-string `serde_json::to_string` temporary of the longest hashed string (`to_vec` starts at 128 B and doubles: ≤ max(128, 2(ε·L + 2)), one at a time), the number-rendering temporaries with the former depth·128 B frame allowance (≤ depth·8·16 + 144 + 128 B), and each object's sorted key vector (`with_capacity(len)` of 8-byte references, live along the current path; charged for every entry at s(&str) = 16 B). That 16 B per entry also covers `keys.sort_by`'s stable-sort scratch: std's stable sort sorts up to 512 references in its 4 KiB stack buffer and otherwise allocates one heap scratch of at most len references (8 B each), freed when `sorted_keys` returns and before the writer descends, so at most one scratch is live and it lies within the charge of the object being sorted (8 B reference + ≤ 8 B scratch per entry; no coefficient change). The SHA-256 state is on the stack. There is no wrapper copy, serialized text, parse tree, seen set, parser scratch or canonical output, and on a borrowed G1 publication no SUCC clone (the successor stays priced as SUCC in W4). Each family's moving becomes the string temporary's previous backing at its longest hashed string (T16 and T17's publication: L_PUB; T17's invocation: the identifier class; T25: the longest of L_PUB, L_BODY, the source identity JSON and the id class); every other T25 owner's old backing is at most about 1 MiB at the caps (the raw request clone's and the factor arrays'), under that 12.3 MB backing at ε = 2. **Guard:** each site's exact text, no former wrapper beside it, and the sha256 of the canonical writer's production code (`validate_checked_value` to its test module); the former forms keep their former prices and any other form stops the run (exit 4). The writer is now `write_canonical` (self-recursive, J6's review) with a non-recursive `write_object_without`, so no new recursion enters the TEXT graph. The removed `source.clone()` in G1 is a pure deletion, so its basis ID audit is pruned by the line map.

The run completes on all four points with no unmapped header, no unclassified argument and both o10 checks at zero. With S5's rules the native evaluation did not fit at the unchanged policy (M = 12 GiB, ⌊0.9M⌋ = 11,596,411,699 B, R = 64 MiB, upward 5 % TEXT reserve). With the T4-S6 rules above all 70 evaluations fit at that policy.

**Registration (T4-S7).** The registered tree is the run at `b4c9f35f8e` (1,516 coefficients, equal to T4-S6's `5981db4ea8` tree but for the basis line; the same 32 pruned rules). The generated block carries the basis line "SQ2 G5 at b4c9f35f8e: the maximum over routes L (C_eq <= 3: worst shape c = 3, z = 0) and E (c = 3), SQ2 rules; text at l <= 128" (307 atoms, 100 forms, no Estimate). `REGISTERED_PROFILES` binds the unchanged build identity and reader layouts, the twelve reviewed inputs' digests read from their actual bytes (#2 and #4–#12 moved; #1 and #3 did not) and `threshold_bytes` = 12,884,901,888. The law tests pin the in-build record, D_env 28,799 and Text(diag_env) 269,494,417; W3 (the T16 publication with the staged copy) is now the in-build maximum in both modes. The merged tree at the native atoms (the in-build collector's 307 atoms plus `Node(String,BTreeMap)` = 648 B) against ⌊0.9M⌋ = 11,596,411,699 B:

| Phase | Dense E + R | Sparse E + R | TEXT reserve | Dense headroom | Sparse headroom |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| W1 | 6,241,772,882 | 6,182,641,538 | 234,404,465 | +5,120,234,352 | +5,179,365,696 |
| W2 | 6,576,151,988 | 6,517,020,644 | 234,404,465 | +4,785,855,246 | +4,844,986,590 |
| W3 | 8,438,143,498 | 8,379,012,154 | 234,404,465 | +2,923,863,736 | +2,982,995,080 |
| W4 | 8,244,976,585 | 8,185,845,241 | 234,404,465 | +3,117,030,649 | +3,176,161,993 |
| W5 | 7,362,099,455 | 7,302,968,111 | 234,404,465 | +3,999,907,779 | +4,059,039,123 |
| X1 | 8,367,548,242 | 8,308,416,898 | 266,412,053 | +2,962,451,404 | +3,021,582,748 |
| X2 | 7,194,089,956 | 7,134,958,612 | 266,412,053 | +4,135,909,690 | +4,195,041,034 |

The tightest evaluation of the 70 is merged dense W3 (+2,923,863,736 B); each unmerged point's tightest is also dense W3 (L at (3,0) +3,479,341,034, (2,1) +4,198,270,782, (1,2) +5,151,748,332; E +3,001,970,473).

### 8.9 O4 actual-source D3 (the Timoshenko and corrosion-allowance source extension)

O4 (DEL-04-08 Design [o4-source-extension.md](o4-source-extension.md)) admits `timoshenko_annulus_energy_v1` documents and nonzero mill tolerance or corrosion allowance on route E (W1-E, `physics-retained-1`) and on the pressure-source route, and publishes each stress row on both section bases. It re-prices by D3 on the source of `060962bcf8` (the reviewed O4 slices). Source pins moved only after the O4 diff of each pinned file was read; the pin comments in each `base/*_model.py` say what changed. Every carried rule was re-keyed by the line map; `linemap.pruned` held the registered run's 32 rules, differing only in carried line numbers, with the same resolutions. Snapshot keys moved with unchanged text were re-keyed (`b7a_owner_choices`, `b7a_returning_errors`, `b7a_phase_model`, `b7a_domain_model`, `b7a_formation_model`, `b7a_scoped_text_model`). Rules added for O4, by label:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **O4-AD** (`b7a_domain_model`, `batch5_model`) | The admission prefix, `family_clauses` and `namespace_branch` are re-pinned: `FamilyFact` loses `ShearModel` and `SectionAllowance`, `contract_is` admits either shear model, and the branch-E section-allowance refusal is deleted. The caps module, census, late admission bodies and every other clause are byte-identical. |
| **O4-L1** (loops) | `reduced_facts`, `distinct_bases` and the attempt's reduced preparations hold at most one entry per member: `distinct_bases` takes at most one push per built pipe, and `reduced_facts` and `reduced_preparations` are reserved at `distinct_bases.len()` and pushed under capacity checks (each checked once in its body). Their loops (`reduced_section_set`, `distinct_copy`, `prepared_not_covered`'s table, `attach_reduced_preparations`) are bounded by m. |
| **O4-L2, O4-L3** (loops) | The case-row scans of `reduced_section_set` and `prepared_not_covered`, and `prepared_alias` with the distinct set, iterate the case's own rows (its scope's block, ≤ P), as U6-L5. `rows_for`'s filter (now keeping a distinct member's reduced rows) is still a filter of `rows_all_for`'s one block (≤ P). `native_bytes`' Timoshenko leaves filter one CaseSource's section terms, one per member (≤ m). |
| **O4-A1, O4-A2** (arguments) | `TimoshenkoLeafError::reason` returns `RETAINED_TIMOSHENKO_REFUSAL` or one of two static literals (read at the snapshot); the selected-maximum warning clones the composite error text it moved before (`error_display`); `stress_operands`' reduced path closure takes one of four static keys (read at the snapshot). |
| **O4-CG1** (call graph) | `prepare_active_case`'s one `prepared` call is the path-qualified `k::TimoshenkoLeaf::prepared` (FK `source.rs`, no allocation); the by-name scan also offered `membrane_publication_range.rs`'s test fixture `prepared`, which rebuilds and validates a model (a false 32-per-case edge that multiplied D by about 28). Only the named definition is the target. |
| **Re-reviewed guards** | J3: `j7_rf_census` is declared `pub(crate)` under the same single `#[cfg(test)]`. B7b-ZA: `section_allowances` converts the allowance, wall and mill tolerance (three `unit_value` sites) in its one visit per pipe. U6-L5: `rows_for`'s new filter. The g5c anchor and both `Self::new(source.source().clone())` sites (the W1 audit at `new_member` and `new_with_shear`). C8: `composite_exact`'s body digest (the second maximum; the borrowed comparison and frame unchanged). The B7a returning-error emitter digest (`retained_w1` carried to `lib.rs:3531`). |

**Positive owners** (`base/o4_owners_model.py`, hooked into `producer_caps.py`, `g4_caps.py`, `t25_g4.py` and `ordinary_caps.py`). Each owner is bound to the sha256 of the bodies that allocate it (24 function bodies and three spans: FK `source.rs`'s sidecar block, `final_case.rs`'s `ReducedSectionSet` and `adaptive.rs`'s `CasePrep::new_with_shear`) and to its exact allocating statements; every file is also under an existing full-file pin. A snapshot without them adds nothing; a changed or half-present body stops the run. No zero and no reuse credit is taken: each owner is charged at the cap with every member Timoshenko and every member's bases distinct (m = 32), at push capacity with its previous backing, on both routes (route L carries none of them, so its charges are dominated by route E's in the merged tree). Native strides come from new in-build atoms in `g5_profile.py` (`ExactMemberBasis`, `ReducedFacts`, `ReducedPreparation`, `ShearAssociation`, the kernel exports `TimoshenkoLeaf` and `ReducedSection`, `(usize, Number)`, `StressComponents`, `(u64, &Value)`), with field-sum SourceUppers for the private `PreparedSectionPatch` and `CompositeMaximum` and the `O4KernelStaging` allowance.
- **Inline kernel growth.** `MemberOperators` (a Timoshenko member's d₁, d₂ and d̄ per plane) and `BoundedCoefficients` (d̄ per plane) carry their D entries inline as `Option`s, at every precision; `ProductCertificateSpent` gains its two reduced capacities. Their in-build strides already price them at every precision and member multiplicity (+374,952 B in W2–W5 at the registered forms).
- **O4-PS** (T11, invocation). `ProductCapture`'s `exact_members` (reserve m), `distinct_bases` (≤ m adapter-copied ids, push-grown), the prepared `TimoshenkoLeafSet` and `reduced_facts`, plus one equal capture-local copy of each (built per case before the equality check).
- **O4-PA** (T12, per attempted case). `prepare_active_case`'s `leaves` and `reduced_facts` locals, the attempt's `reduced_preparations` and `shear_associations` (held in `AttemptParts` to serialization, each a full `SectionPreparationWork` record), the `CasePrep`'s cloned sidecar, and the replaced K4SRC identity live while `new_with_shear` computes its successor (one more E_src).
- **O4-ID.** A non-empty sidecar frames a block into every K4SRC and K4STF identity (version byte 0x02, `TSH\x01`, u32 count, 12-byte entries): 8 + 12m = 392 B more in E_src and E_stf wherever either is held (preparation, evidence, source origins).
- **O4-RS, O4-NC, O4-PP, O4-KS** (T14, per attempted case). The `ReducedSectionSet` (m sections with 21 row indices each, the stable sort's scratch and `new`'s `seen` vector at push capacity of 21m with its previous backing); the reduced-row index maps (`reserve::<u32>(P_final)`; two live at most, charged twice) and reduced-coverage bitmaps (21m, twice); `prepared_not_covered`'s reduced operand tables (one `[[Option<usize>;5];5]` per distinct member, as U4B-W1's table) and bound-position bitmap (P_final); `distinct_copy`'s ids; the reduced maxima (`MaximumE` m → 2m); the frozen payload's reduced sections and shear leaves; and one `O4KernelStaging` SourceUpper of 64 Wide<16> for the kernel's new per-member stack values (the κ_E and D-entry enclosures of both lanes, the η and bridge D-entry gaps, `timoshenko_entries`' operands). Stack values are not compiler-frame measurements.
- **O4-TR** (T15, per attempted case). A distinct member's reduced annulus preparation is a second preparation record: `PreparedMemberEvent` m → 2m and its nine `ConversionEvent`s 9m → 18m.
- **O4-WR** (receipt; every BODY, SUCC and publication copy). Each source section term gains `shear_area` and `reduced` {area, section_modulus, geometry{route, normalized_od, effective_wall, actual_radius, actual_second_moment, actual_polar_moment}}; each selection term `shear_area` and `reduced` {area, section_modulus}; each member's preparation record `reduced`, charged as a second full member record with its nine conversions. The distinct members' 21 reduced rows per member each take a class entry (`absolute_verified`): the row premise P already counts them as published rows (U4B-RP), so this is a conservative second charge. The preparation payload (C3) copies no reduced record and is unchanged.
- **O4-RD** (T17). `rows_for`'s, `g5c`'s and `g5b_exact_evidence`'s distinct-member sets (≤ m borrowed ids each, three at once), `native_bytes`' Timoshenko leaves (collected, push capacity with its previous backing, per source and stiffness encoding) and the block it appends to each rebuilt K4SRC/K4STF buffer (`G8_BYTES`), and `physics_source`'s per-case model set (≤ m inserts; also in T25). G8's rederivation of both bases and the shear-area leaf (`section_basis::reduced_wall`, `timoshenko_shear_factor`, `exact_material`) is scalar work with error-only texts in TAV.
- **O4-SR** (ordinary recovery O and T25). A selected case's member whose stress sections differ takes a second `composite_member_maximum` on its reduced section, so the reduced `CompositeMaximum` (its value and evidence Value, one `pipe_stress_extrema` entry of the census, `MAXIMUM_EVIDENCE`) lives beside the nominal one, one member at a time, in the case solve and in `composite_exact`.
- **O4-SP** (T25). The private payload's `formation_inputs.shear_area_bits` and `reduced_recovery_section` {four bits} per frame; the plan's longer reduced operand paths (one 64-byte path more per reduced row, conservative); `derived_for`'s per-station `Vec` of the two bases' `StressComponents`.
- **O4-EV** (O, every envelope copy). A Timoshenko member's evidence `beam_theory` adds two number entries beyond the census entry (§9, "Gate bounds"), charged in the ordinary evidence, the successor envelope, the staged copy and T25's publication.
- **TEXT.** The chain's inventory prices O4's new and edited literals (the restated limitation strings among them), the deletions of the three decline texts, `MILL_TOLERANCE_DECLINE` and `SOURCE_MAXIMUM_BASES_DISTINCT`, and the new by-name text paths (O4's new call sites into existing text-allocating helpers). `RETAINED_TIMOSHENKO_REFUSAL` stays as the association text.

The run completes on all four points with no unmapped header, no unclassified argument and both o10 checks at zero; all 70 evaluations fit at the unchanged policy (M = 12 GiB, ⌊0.9M⌋ = 11,596,411,699 B, R = 64 MiB, upward 5 % TEXT reserve). No product source changed for pricing.

### 8.11 W1 coverage repair actual-source D3

The W1 coverage repair ([w1-coverage-repair.md](w1-coverage-repair.md) §2.2 and §6) re-prices its implementation head `3bbfdd5080` at `7299e6319d`, the commit that carries the pins and adds the rules below; its product source equals `3bbfdd5080`. Against 5a's `6b90748dec` the priced source moves in four ways:
- the frame kernel's loaded-maximum `search` takes `driven`. When set, it runs on `Enclosure::point(z.lo)` and makes one more `point_max` per evaluated point for `lower_hull`, inside the unchanged loop and limits. `recipe_reduced` passes `corner1() && ..`, and `corner1()` is false without `census-probe`, so the driven path is compiled but not taken in the registered build. It is priced anyway;
- `check_intervals` returns the coupled, floored per-body scales it gated with: the vector `row_scales_reduced` reserves exactly at `body_count` (`capacities[6]`). `certify_final` moves them into `CertifiedProductProof.final_scales`. Nothing new is allocated; the backing now lives until the proof drops after serialization;
- `retained_wire.rs` `selection` borrows the certified proof and writes `body_scales` from `final_scales()`;
- `census_probe.rs`, its driver `w1_census_driver.rs` and feature-gated hooks in `loaded_maximum.rs`, `final_case.rs`, `source_residual.rs`, `structural.rs`, `origins.rs` and `retained_memory.rs`, all under `census-probe` (W1R-FG).

**Pins and carried rules.** Every changed span was read before re-pinning. The full-file pins move:
- `retained_wire.rs` `559f4d0a…` → `154ab751…` (`b7a_domain_model.py`, `w1_member_load_model.py`, `w1_reductions_model.py`);
- `structural.rs` `52c48f5e…` → `10651e4d…` (`b7a_formation_model.py`);
- in `w1_member_load_model.py`, `origins.rs` `fc139cb2…` → `545462fc…`, `final_case.rs` `ed9ff38d…` → `ea2ba06b…`, `loaded_maximum.rs` `cc9ad2a9…` → `ec18ecd2…` and `source_residual.rs` `6208ea03…` → `e2ef2ea1…`.

`o4_owners_model.py` re-pins `selection`; the section terms O4-WR prices are unchanged. `structural.rs` gains six `retained_api` lines above every snapshot key in `b7a_owner_choices.py`, so those keys carry +6 with unchanged text and caller inventories: `load_fidelity` 548 → 554, `audit_load_fidelity` 1226 → 1232, `row_sources` 1287 → 1293 with its clone site 1288 → 1294, `unaudited_row` 1295 → 1301 and `audit_load_row` 1324 → 1330. The line map carries every rule (869 remaps, no unmapped key). Its 32 pruned rules and two reviewed resolutions are §8.10's. Rules added, by label:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **W1R-FG** (`loopscan.py`, feature-gated source) | The registered build identity is built with no features, so code under `census-probe` is not compiled into it and cannot allocate there. The scanners treat it as they treat `#[cfg(test)]` items, and as they already treat `#[cfg(any(test, ..))]` for `mutation-controls`. Items and statements under exactly `#[cfg(feature = "census-probe")]` or `#[cfg(all(test, feature = "census-probe"))]` are blanked, with line numbers kept, in the call graph and the template inventory. A `#[cfg(not(feature = "census-probe"))]` arm is compiled in and stays priced. The call graph, the template inventory and JN1's inventory skip `census_probe.rs` and `w1_census_driver.rs`. The build includes them only through compiled-out `#[path]` declarations: the gated one in `final_case.rs`, and the driver's two in the census test files. **Guards** (exit 4): another inclusion, a plain module, a new off-build file, or any manifest line naming the feature other than the kernel's `census-probe = []` and `product_physics`' forwarding entry. The last covers a `default` or an enabling dependency. A zero needs a premise: here it is the cfg and the manifest audit. Pricing the probe as live would charge thread-local record accumulation that the registered build cannot perform and no D1 bound covers. The source was read with the repair. **Limit:** the identity string records no features, so a `census-probe` build reports the registered identity. Only census commands build it, and `census_probe_is_off_in_the_ordinary_test_build` asserts that the ordinary test build has it off. |
| **W1R-FS** (`w1r_owners_model.py`, positive owner) | Per attempted case (T14, live through W2–W5 with T12–T15), the proof's `final_scales` backing is one `[f64; 4]` per body at the exact reserve, at most n = 32 bodies. Each proof holder is also one `Vec` header larger: the attempt's held record, plus six frames on the case chain counted twice each for local and return slot (`certify_final`, `freeze_case_certify`, `freeze_case_settle`, `freeze_case`'s closure, `freeze_case` and the attempt closure). That is 13 headers; the combination chain holds fewer. T14.3's existing charge of the transient scales (`BodyCoverage`) stays, so no reuse credit is taken. The owner is bound to the bodies of `row_scales_reduced`, `check_intervals`, `certify_final` and `selection`, the `CertifiedProductProof` span and the exact moving statements. A snapshot without the field adds nothing; a changed body stops. |
| **W1R-LM** (`w1_member_load_model.loaded_inventory`) | The driven search's frame holds the represented hull (`hull_z`, two endpoints) beside its z_lo point, plus `lower_hull` and the named `point` value. These four endpoint slots are added to the rounded union rather than assumed inside it: 1,022,528 B, still within the 1 MiB L4 charge, so no coefficient moves. The second `point_max` runs after the first returns, as a sequential callee frame with no allocation. |
| **W1R-L1** (`o10_rules.py`, loop) | `selection`'s `scales.iter().enumerate().map` runs over the final scales, one per body (`body_count` ≤ n). It replaces the loop over the native `BTreeMap` of the same bodies, which had the same bound. |
| **Existing rules** | The driven search's extra `point_max` evaluations run inside the existing loop under the same limits. The by-name graph already holds `search` → `point_max`, which allocates nothing and formats no text. `selection`'s new refusal `cases[].selection.body_scales` is a `&'static str` path. No reduction is taken for the `BTreeMap` that `selection` no longer builds. TAV_W and TAV_X are unchanged at every point. |

The run at `7299e6319d` completes on all four points with no unmapped header or unclassified argument, and both o10 checks are at zero. `compare` against 5a's tree, reproduced by its committed tool at `6b90748dec` (exit 0; `generate --check` matches §8.10's registered block), gives `a_le_b` false. 1,558 coefficients are equal and 4 rise: `T14` and `success__T14` `s(f64)` 12,288 → 12,672 and `s(Vec)` 18 → 57. Each of the four point trees rises the same way. ### 8.13 R-A actual-source D3

R-A, the source route's work reduction ([source-work-nondyadic-inputs.md](source-work-nondyadic-inputs.md) §3.1 and §4, "D3"), re-prices at `7d946b7e94`, the commit that carries the pins and adds the rules below. Its product source equals `2dcba5ab08`, the implementation after its review repairs. Against `9e03991d39` (§8.12's product source) the priced source moves in four ways:
- FK `exact_boundary/functionals.rs`: `FunctionalPlan` gains `hints: Vec<Option<u32>>`. `new_with_hints` takes a `HintRule` (`&dyn Fn(usize) -> Option<u32>`), and `new` and `new_with_hints` route through a private `plan`. The new `build_hints` charges 2 × the descriptor count and then collects `(0..count).map(rule)`. `RetainedFunctionalSet::replay_against` takes `Option<HintRule>` and builds its own hint vector after the same charge. `evaluate` takes the hints and a shortcut flag (both product callers pass `true`) and holds R-A's `Shortcuts` (`mod shortcuts`): B2's `zero`, `window` and `products` (n entries each), the push-grown `touched` and B1's `source_ok` (reserved at the descriptor count). A B1 row's value is the source row's Ratio with its numerator negated termwise (an exact-size `collect`) and its denominator cloned;
- PP `source_recovery.rs`: `Sources::hint` is B1's index rule, arithmetic on the descriptor index with no allocation. `solve`'s plan and `replay_against` pass it to the kernel as a borrowed closure. `j7_rf_census.rs` declares the `#[cfg(test)]` gating module `ra_gating`;
- FK `exact_operation_count.rs` gains R-A's row trace (`RowPath`, `take_row_paths`), and `functionals.rs` gains the long reference entry `evaluate_functionals_long`, `FunctionalSet::ratios` and `Shortcuts::skipped`. All of these are under `#[cfg(any(test, feature = "exact-operation-count"))]`, and so is the module `exact_operation_count` itself. The scanners blank them as test items (`TEST_ATTR`, §8.11's W1R-FG text), so they are not priced. See the paragraph on the row trace below;
- test modules (`shortcut_tests.rs`, `ra_gating_tests.rs`, the metered tests) and FK's `Cargo.toml` comment.

**Pins and carried rules.** Every changed span was read before re-pinning. One full-file pin moves: `source_recovery.rs` `e6a6cfee…` → `04b56239…` (`b7a_formation_model.py`), because `Sources::hint` and the two hint-rule call sites add no formed-load or finding owner. No other pin moves. PP `lib.rs`, `source_receipt.rs`, `source_receipt/source.rs` and FK `structural.rs` are byte-identical to §8.12's source, and so is `exact_boundary.rs`, which `rf_source_model.py` reads. The line map carries every rule (869 remaps, no unmapped key). Its 32 pruned rules and two reviewed resolutions are §8.12's. Fifteen carried keys move with unchanged text: the fourteen `source_recovery.rs` ID audits by +15 (for example 1456 → 1471 and 423 → 438; 1922 → 1937 carries two keys), and the `loop_bounds` `edge_zero[41]` callee `functionals.rs` `len` 744 → 828. The new loops reach no TEXT site, so the loop inventory lists none of them. Their bounds belong to the owners below: `prepare_sources` gains no loop; `build_hints` runs over the descriptor count; B2's `start` runs over the prescribed list and the block DOFs (≤ n each); `begin_row` runs over the row's terms (≤ the descriptor bound 144m + s + l) and over `touched` (≤ n); B1's extra-term scan runs over `ds[j − component ..= j]` (≤ component + 1 ≤ 6 rows of PP's descriptors), and its static comparisons run over the two rows' terms and atoms. None of these loop bodies allocates. Rules added, by label:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **RA-HS** (`ra_owners_model.py`, positive owner) | A B1 hint vector is `build_hints`' `(0..count).map(rule).collect()`. A mapped `Range` has an exact length, so the capacity is exactly the descriptor count, at most Fn = 42m + N + s + 6g (1,760 at the caps), each a native `Option<u32>` (the new in-build atom `s(Option<u32>)`, 8 B). Two exist. The plan's is held in `FunctionalPlan` until the plan drops, in T07 E3 per attempted case. The replay's is built in `replay_against` and held through the replay's evaluation, in T25.1f. PP holds none, and the plan is not cloned (`FunctionalPlan` is not `Clone`). |
| **RA-SC** (`ra_owners_model.py`, positive owner) | One evaluation's `Shortcuts`, at n = c.f.len() = F DOFs: `zero` (n bools), `window` (n u8), `products` (n usize), `touched` and `source_ok`. `touched` is a push-grown `usize` list that gets at most one entry per DOF in a row (pushed only from window state 0, and reset by the next row's `drain`, which keeps the capacity), so ≤ n live and 8·pushcap(n) B with its previous backing as a moving candidate. `source_ok` is `with_capacity(ds.len())`, with exactly one push per row (B1's `push(false)` or `note`'s), so it is never reallocated (Fn B). That is 5,728 B at the caps, in T07 F3 for the live evaluation and in T25.1f for the replay's. B1's negated Ratio is the row's value. Its numerator (an exact-size `collect`) and its cloned denominator are each at most the 256-term expansion cap, so they lie within T07 F1's per-value charge of two full expansions. **Guard:** the bodies of `build_hints`, `new_with_hints`, `plan`, `evaluate` and `replay_against` (FK), and of `hint`, `prepare_sources`, `solve`, `replay_against` and `check_binding_against` (PP), are pinned by sha256. So are the `FunctionalPlan`, `mod shortcuts` and `Sources` spans, the 22 exact allocating, moving and borrowing statements (each once), and `mod shortcuts`' inventory of allocating constructs (three `vec![`, one `Vec::with_capacity`, one `.collect()`, one `.clone()`, and no `.to_vec()`, `String`, `format!` or `Box::new`). A snapshot without R-A adds nothing; a changed or partly present owner stops the run. |
| **RA-EDGE** (`O10_EDGE_ZERO`) | `publication_predicate(&h, row, scale, meter)` in adaptive.rs `certify_rows` passes the loop's pattern binding `((row, meta), v)`, a published row, not a function. The by-name function-as-value scan picks R-A's row-trace recorder `fn row` in `exact_boundary/exact_operation_count.rs`. That module is declared only under `#[cfg(any(test, feature = "exact-operation-count"))]` and adaptive.rs never names it. Without the rule the false edge made `certify_rows`' row loop an unmapped header on every point. Only that one edge is zeroed, in J2's form; `certify_rows`' other edges stay. **Guard:** the call site occurs once in `certify_rows`; `exact_boundary.rs` declares the module once, under that attribute; adaptive.rs does not name it. |

**The row trace in test builds** (`exact-operation-count`). `core/product_physics/Cargo.toml` enables that feature for FK through `[dev-dependencies]` (RF §5.4), so every PP test build compiles it. That includes the law tests', the challenge binary's and the stack witnesses' builds, which carry the registered identity. R-A's row trace is the only heap owner under the feature, and it allocates only while its thread's `RECORDING` flag is set (`9ac1490b3a`). `begin_evaluation` and `row` push only when the flag is set, and only `take_row_paths` sets it. The gating tests (`shortcut_tests.rs`, `ra_gating_tests.rs`) are the only callers of `take_row_paths`, so in every other PP test build the trace is a thread-local flag read that allocates nothing. Builds without dev-dependencies do not compile it. Blanking the gated code (`TEST_ATTR`) therefore omits no allocation of a registered-identity run outside those tests. Re-priced at `9937d08af9`, which merges the fix, the tree is coefficient-identical to `7d946b7e94`'s on all four points and merged, and `generate --check` equals the registered block with its basis line.

The run at `7d946b7e94` completes on all four points with no unmapped header or unclassified argument, and both o10 checks are at zero. `compare` against §8.12's tree, reproduced by its committed tool (the tool at `9e03991d39` priced `aefefa5043` with exit 0, and `generate --check` matches §8.12's block as committed at `9e03991d39`), gives `a_le_b` false and `a_ge_b` true. 1,556 coefficients are equal and 12 rise, the forms `O_base_dense`, `O_base_sparse` and `T25_S1` with their `success__` copies:
- `O_base_*[1]`: 257,954,808 → 257,971,992 (dense) and 236,595,288 → 236,612,472 (sparse), +17,184 = 3 × 5,728 B (RA-SC per attempted case);
- `O_base_*[s(Option<u32>)]`: 0 → 5,280 = 3 × 1,760 (RA-HS, the plan's hints per attempted case);
- `T25_S1[1]`: 24,550,547 → 24,556,275 (+5,728, the replay's Shortcuts) and `T25_S1[s(Option<u32>)]`: 0 → 1,760 (the replay's hints).

Each point tree rises in the same 12 coefficients. D and TAV_W/TAV_X are unchanged at every point. At native atoms every phase of every tree rises by O_base's 59,424 B in both modes; T25_S1 does not set a phase maximum. All 70 native evaluations fit at the unchanged policy: M = 12,884,901,888 B, ⌊0.9M⌋ = 11,596,411,699 B, R = 64 MiB and upward-rounded 5 % TEXT reserve.

**Registration.** The generated block's basis is "SQ2 G5 at 7d946b7e94: the maximum over routes L (C_eq <= 3: worst shape c = 3, z = 0) and E (c = 3), SQ2 rules; text at l <= 128". It has 320 atoms, 100 forms, 26 SourceUpper and no Estimate (1,568 coefficients). The in-build collector supplies all 320 values (rustc 1.97.1, debug, opt-level 0, no RUSTFLAGS, no features). The 319 earlier atoms are as in §8.12, and `s(Option<u32>)` = 8 B is new. Route L's other shapes also use the same-build `Node(String,BTreeMap)` = 648 B (§8.9). `REGISTERED_PROFILES` keeps the exact build identity, reader layouts and `threshold_bytes` = 12,884,901,888. All twelve reviewed-input digests were recomputed from actual bytes and are unchanged. `generate --check` reproduces the block. Two numerical pins move, each by +59,424 B:
- the law tests' phase record, every phase in both modes (W3 is still the maximum);
- the challenge binary's `W1_PHASE_BYTES` (6,205,036,592 and 6,264,167,936) and `MAX_PHASE_BYTES` (8,435,703,463 and 8,494,834,807).

D_env 28,799, Text(diag_env) 269,494,417, binding counts, policy, layouts and digests are unchanged. The `profile_` and `retained_memory` law tests and the default `retained_memory_challenge` test pass in the registered build. All six AC-004 stack-headroom witnesses (`witness_headroom_w1_at_one_mebibyte`, `witness_w2_deep_milestone_publishes` and `witness_w_c2_publishes`, sparse and dense, at R/64 = 1 MiB) pass in the registered build, one process each under the host's exclusive lock; they report pass or fail, not a smallest passing stack.

### 8.14 Pre-0.4 failure unification actual-source D3

The pre-0.4 failure unification ([pre04-failure-unification.md](pre04-failure-unification.md) §2.2 and §6, "D3") re-prices at `64144ab20b`, the commit that carries the pins and adds the rules below. Its product source equals `b6cc3a792a`. The comparison basis is `ac1f57a207` (main `ca97f93f3b` with the design text): its committed tool prices it with exit 0, and `generate --check` reproduces §8.13's registered block with §8.13's basis line. Against it the priced source moves in five ways:
- PP `lib.rs`: `run_linear_static_preview_captured` republishes every captured exact-route invocation whose selected source could not finalize. The new `republication` runs `run_linear_static_preview_captured_once` once more on the same ledger with the join withheld, taking its request from `CapturedInvocation::requested` after `fail_closed_form` has reduced the first envelope. `permitted_run` does the same after dropping the observer (with its permit) and returns `Coexistence`. S1, S3 and S4 record the cause on both routes; selection screens by the captured replay's reservation and declines a withheld join on both routes; S2 records `support {id} norm: {error}` and S3 fails with it; `source_row_bindings` reports a missing row. The budget's two fields are renamed (`join_withheld`, `join_failure`). The test-only screen switch is under `#[cfg(test)]` and blanked as test code (`TEST_ATTR`);
- PP `source_receipt.rs`: `requested` is `pub(super)` with a doc comment, and the fallback test module is `pub(crate)`;
- PP `source_recovery.rs`: the screen's and `decline_withheld`'s doc comments, and RF-14's restated test;
- FK `exact_boundary.rs`: one doc comment (no pin; the line map carries its keys);
- test modules (`load_state_fallback_tests.rs`, `retained_facade_tests.rs`, `s11g_tests.rs`).

**Pins and carried rules.** Every changed span was read before re-pinning. Three full-file pins move: `lib.rs` `70cd91d5…` → `e8185273…` (`b7a_domain_model.py`, `w1_reductions_model.py`'s reduced cohort and `kd5_text_model.py`'s KD5C4 binding, which still finds no added or removed `.min(`/`.max(` call), `source_receipt.rs` `dbb32f92…` → `9ee47bc1…` (`b7a_domain_model.py`, `lib/b7a_retired_carry.py`) and `source_recovery.rs` `04b56239…` → `cf68c6e2…` (`b7a_formation_model.py`). `solve_prepared_load_case`'s body pin moves in `kd5_text_model.py`, `kd5_first_error_model.py` and `batch5_model.py` (`e11b74db…` → `96aab837…`) and in `o4_owners_model.py` (`7aab4608…` → `9d7a4c2a…`): its record constructors, labels, route strings, carriers, staging, fallback attempts and first-return alternatives are unchanged. Snapshot keys moved with unchanged text: in `lib.rs` by +26 to +103 (`b7a_domain_model.py`'s nineteen audit keys, `b7a_owner_choices.py`'s four helper keys, `b7a_phase_model.py`'s `solve_prepared_load_case` 4687 → 4772 and `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner` 2572 → 2634, `b7a_returning_errors.py`'s owners, sites and callers, `b7a_scoped_text_model.py`'s four lines); in `source_receipt.rs` by +4 (`b7a_phase_model.py`'s T25 caller inventory, 657/743/759 → 661/747/763); and the carried `retained_w1` key in `sens.py`'s `_X` emitter (3531 → 3616), whose hash `49accb86…` joins `b7a_returning_errors.py`'s `MODE_SOURCE`. The line map carries every rule (869 remaps, no unmapped key); its 32 pruned rules and two reviewed resolutions are §8.13's. One basis key constrains the source: the S3 record line `source_budget.record_load_state_join_failure(format!(` (basis `lib.rs:5744`) holds an ID audit, so the budget's two methods keep their 0.4-era names and only the fields are renamed. The tool's own tests follow the moved keys, and `tests/test_pu_republication_phase.py` covers PU-X3's selection. Rules added, by label:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **PU-TX** (`o10_rules.py`, guard) | The cause templates are read at the snapshot, each once: S2 `support {} norm: {}` (15 literal bytes), S1 `captured source context: {}` (25), S3 `case {}: {}` (7), S4 `invocation receipt: {}` (20) and the withheld text (106, 0.4's bytes). A changed template stops the run. |
| **PU-ID** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`) | The two new format sites' identifier arguments are priced as their siblings are: the S2 cause's `support.id` (IN128), and the missing-row text's `entity` (IN128), `kind` and `location` (static, 128), as on the "not unique" line beside it. Each site keeps its full multiplicity: the missing-row site is inside the descriptor loop (5,280 at E's caps), as its sibling is. No first-error reduction is claimed. |
| **PU-S2** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`) | A selected case's S3 sites read `error.0`, which may now be the S2 cause, so `error.0` at the S3 cause record and the S3 finalization diagnostic is bounded by the S2 site's own size, 15 + 128 + 8,192 = 8,335 B, not by one error Display (8,192). |
| **PU-C** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`) | The recorded cause is one of the S1, S3 and S4 record sites, the largest being S3 carrying the S2 cause: 7 + 128 + 8,335 = 8,470 B. That bounds `cause` in the withheld text, and `withheld` in the case's `SOURCE_BLOCK_RECOVERY_UNAVAILABLE` message is bounded by 106 + 8,470 = 8,576 B. On 0.4 these sites were excluded (D1.3); on route E they are now reachable. |
| **PU-CL** (`O10_ARGS_FIRST`) | The three clones of the recorded cause (`source_budget.join_failure`, `budget.join_failure`, `self.join_failure`: the captured route's and the Direct entry's checks and the republication's ledger copy) are each one cause, ≤ 8,470 B. |
| **PU-R1** (`o10_rules.py`, guard of G4's one-run cap) | `loop_bounds`' `run_linear_static_preview_observed` cap 1 now means at most one ordinary run live at a time. The guard requires, in `run_linear_static_preview_captured`, one first run and one `republication` call, in the order `fail_closed_form(envelope)`, `capture.requested()`, `drop(fail_closed)`, `republication(`; in `republication`, one run on `withholding_load_state_join(cause)`; and in `permitted_run`, one observed run, then `fail_closed_form(ordinary)`, `drop(observer)`, `capture.requested()`, `drop(fail_closed)`, `republication(` and `Err(W1Fallback::Coexistence)`. Before the second run starts the first run has returned and its envelope and, on the Direct entry, its observer have dropped. Only the capture, the ledger and the recorded cause persist, and each is already priced (R_raw and T_resident in O_base, the cause texts in TAV at their own sites). The edge-once rule on `run_linear_static_preview_captured` → `run_linear_static_preview_captured_once` holds trivially: the function now makes one direct call. |
| **PU-X3** (`b7a_phase_model.py`, `g4_caps.py`; phase model) | The republication is its own phase, X3, "the second ordinary span after a selected source could not finalize": requested O_base + TAV_X + every phase's resident owners (T11, T19, STATICS, FAILED_MANIFEST_COPY, FORMATION_VALUE_BUILD, W1_MEMBER_READER), moving TXT, HELPER and T07, as W1's. Its attempts are live T07 attempts that are then declined, so nothing is selected: no T25 runs, and W1 does not start (`Coexistence`), so no W phase follows it. The request it deserializes from the capture occupies T_resident (which includes the parse's raw-clone transient). During that deserialization, the first run's fail-closed form is at most a blocked envelope (ErrorPrefix in O_base, its text within TAV) or a source-labelled shell without rows, evidence or diagnostics. Its text is the full TAV_X, as in X1 and X2; T11 stays resident although the observer has dropped. X3 is added only where `lib.rs` defines both `republication` and `fail_closed_form`; a partial source stops the run, and earlier sources keep seven phases. The runtime law, the evaluator and the 5 % reserve (TAV_X for every X phase) read the phase list from the generated block. |

The run at `64144ab20b` completes on all four points with no unmapped header or unclassified argument, and both o10 checks are at zero. `compare` against §8.13's tree as reproduced at `ac1f57a207` gives `a_le_b` false and `a_ge_b` true (structure differs by X3). 1,536 coefficients are equal and 32 rise: fourteen forms with their `success__` copies, and four text atoms.

### 8.15 Silent-zero guard

The silent-zero guard (DEL-04-07 [pressure-contract.md](../../DEL-04-07/Design/pressure-contract.md), "Load cases that apply nothing") re-prices at its product source `7a81b247ed`. The comparison basis is main `9ad5d79fa5`: the committed tool prices it with exit 0 (the SZ rules add nothing there), and `generate --check` reproduces §8.14's registered block with §8.14's basis line. Against it the priced source moves in two ways:
- PP `validation.rs`, `validate_required_collections`: a load-state model returns before the load checks (`case_state::resolve` judges its cases); an explicit `pressure_regions: []` applies nothing; and, unless the model has a constant-effort support or an objective connector (`model_loads_every_case`), each authored case that applies nothing among loaded ones gets a `LOAD_CASE_APPLIES_NO_ACTION` warning, in a loop over the load cases;
- PP `case_state/resolve.rs`, `resolve_case`: `LOAD_STATE_CASE_APPLIES_NO_ACTION` (blocking, through `block`) when a case's stored primitives are all unlisted and it applies no other action, and the `LOAD_STATE_SOURCE_EXCLUDED` warning otherwise when some are unlisted; both name at most `formation_guard::NAMED` = 6 primitive ids.

**Pins and carried rules.** Neither file is pinned, and no pinned file or body moves (`case_state/mod.rs` keeps `633b46de…` in `b7a_domain_model.py`, `formation_guard.rs` keeps `5c67a95e…`). The line map carries every rule with no unmapped key; its 32 pruned rules and two reviewed resolutions are main's, entry for entry. At the head the run was incomplete on every point with three unclassified arguments, all at the validation site: its message, the id format's `stable_suffix(&case.id)` and the refs' `case.id`. The guard's message is a 198-byte static literal, within the template inventory's 200-character cut, so the inventory prices it at its own bytes. The loop `for case in model.load_cases.iter().filter(|case| case_applies_nothing(case))` is already bound at `cases` (lc) by the inherited per-case rule. Rules added, by label:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **SZ-TX** (`o10_rules.py`, guard) | In `validate_required_collections`, read at the snapshot: one loop over the load cases that apply nothing, one `LOAD_CASE_APPLIES_NO_ACTION` diag with the id format `diagnostic:load-case:{}:applies-no-action` over `stable_suffix(&case.id)`, one static message literal, refs `vec![case.id.clone()]`, and four `diag(` sites in the function. A changed site stops the run. |
| **SZ-ID** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`) | `stable_suffix(&case.id)` is an authored case id with `:` replaced by `-`, the same length, IN128; the refs' `case.id` clone is IN128. They are priced as the sibling per-case sites in `validation.rs`. The site keeps its full multiplicity, once per authored case per call of `validate_required_collections` (2·lc). No credit is taken for the `else` branch, where at least one case is loaded and so at most lc − 1 cases are empty, or for its exclusion of `LOAD_INPUT_MISSING`. |
| **SZ-LS** (`O10_MULT0_SITES`) | `resolve_case` runs only under `case_state::is_load_state` (the `load_state` branch of the ordinary run and the captured load-state replay). D1.3 refuses every authored load/reference-state field on every branch (`retained_memory.rs` `family_clauses`: `ReferenceConfigurations`, `MaterialExpansionLaw`, `RequestExpansionLaws`, and per case `AnalysisState`), and route L has no exact contract, so `is_load_state` is false on both routes (B7b-LS) and the base chain's `case_state/` file zero holds. The new findings' sites (the `LOAD_STATE_SOURCE_EXCLUDED` diag, the refusal's `format!` and the named-ids join) are guarded at multiplicity 0 rather than assumed. The early return in `validate_required_collections` is therefore never taken on D1 either; it allocates nothing. |

`tests/test_silent_zero_guard.py` covers SZ-ID's and SZ-LS's keys at the current source and SZ-TX's stop. `test_b7b_exclusive_diagnostics.py` now reads B7b-JR's own `joint.rs` entry among the multiplicity-0 sites.

The run at `7a81b247ed` completes on all four points with no unmapped header or unclassified argument, and both o10 checks are at zero. `compare` against main's tree gives `a_le_b` false and `a_ge_b` true (structure equal). 1,470 coefficients are equal and 98 rise; none fall. The warning adds 6 diagnostics at E's and L(3,0)'s caps (D 76,375 → 76,381; 43,418 → 43,424) and 3 to the envelope (D_env 28,799 → 28,802; PushCap(D_env) stays 32,768). Each warning is 2,398 B (2,048 fixed, the 198-byte message, 152 B of refs).
- **`TAV_W[1]` and `TAV_X[1]`.** 4,783,595,226 → 4,783,627,254 (+32,028 B) and 5,366,446,372 → 5,366,462,386 (+16,014 B). `TAV_text_requested` and `TAV_text_moving` rise by 32,028 B. At E's caps the rise is the warning's sites, 30,492 B (the diag with its message 26,952 B, the id format 2,004 B and the refs clone 1,536 B), and `stable_suffix`'s six more calls, 1,536 B. L(3,0)'s TAV rises by 57,240 B, which adds the preview evidence reader's per-diagnostic text (25,212 B). L(2,1)'s rises by 38,160 B and L(1,2)'s by 19,080 B.

The `[1]` and per-node coefficients of FAILED_MANIFEST_COPY, STAGED, SUCC, BODY, T16_P1–P4, T17_V2/V4–V6 and T25_I1–I3 rise with them, and NOTICE_moving's and STAGED's `s(Diagnostic)` with D_env.

- **`model_loads_every_case`.** It allocates no text. Its `.any` closures over supports, the connector components (empty by P2), `q_ref`'s keys and rods reach text only through by-name edges to unrelated `get` functions, none of which holds a TEXT site. The rule guards its source: one call, over `model.components`, and no other caller. `joint.rs`'s reached functions and multiplicities are then those of `074cd8b67c`.

The runs at `074cd8b67c` and, with SZ-CP, `21fa9116df` complete on all four points with both o10 checks at zero.
