### 8.16 W-8 topology warnings actual-source D3

W-8's pre-solve topology warnings ([model-topology-checks.md](../../DEL-04-06/Design/model-topology-checks.md)) re-price at `34e96d6b17`, the commit that carries the pins and adds the rules below. Its product source equals `97e6fda00f`, W-8 after its independent review (a first pricing at `b8b236e2ef`, of the pre-review source, was superseded). The comparison basis is main's registered tree. Main `44c31ab439` changes no priced crate's `src` and no tool file since `7ded624835`, whose committed tool prices it with exit 0; `generate --check` reproduces §8.14's registered block with §8.14's basis line. Against it the priced source moves in four ways:
- PP `lib.rs`: `mod topology;` and one call, `topology::append_topology_diagnostics(&model, &built, &mut diagnostics)`, after the build in `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner`. It runs once per ordinary run on both routes; X3's republication runs it again after the first run has dropped, inside X3's own O_base;
- PP `topology.rs` (new): `element_edges`; `disconnected_parts`, with a second `Bodies`, the part map, three sets and one MODEL_TOPOLOGY_DISCONNECTED warning naming at most NAMED = 6 parts; and `near_duplicate_nodes`, with a cell grid two tolerances wide, the 27-neighbour scan into a per-node `found` buffer sorted by the other node, the first NAMED coincident and near pairs with their counts, and NODE_COINCIDENT and NODE_NEAR_DUPLICATE, each naming at most 6 pairs. `scaled_norm` replaces the squared-norm square roots, and coincidence is an exactly zero difference. Each code yields at most one warning per call, and all three can fire together;
- PP `validation.rs`: `validate_components` builds a node-degree map over the pipe segments before its component loop, and inside that loop it adds the BRANCH_TOPOLOGY_INVALID warning (`branch_topology_invalid`) for a component that names a node;
- a runtime test (`tests/topology_checks_runtime.rs`).

**Pins and carried rules.** Every changed span was read before re-pinning. One full-file pin moves: `lib.rs` `e8185273…` → `17d2d1d5…` (`b7a_domain_model.py`, `w1_reductions_model.py`'s reduced cohort and `kd5_text_model.py`'s KD5C4 binding). The two added lines add no `.min(` or `.max(` call and change no owner these models price. Snapshot keys moved with unchanged text, in `lib.rs` by +1 before the call and +2 after it: `b7a_domain_model.py`'s nineteen audit keys, `b7a_owner_choices.py`'s four helper keys, `b7a_phase_model.py`'s `solve_prepared_load_case` (4772 → 4774) and `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner` (2634 → 2635), `b7a_returning_errors.py`'s owners, sites and callers, and `b7a_scoped_text_model.py`'s four lines. The carried `retained_w1` key in `sens.py`'s `_X` emitter moves 3616 → 3618, and its hash `779a5d30…` joins `b7a_returning_errors.py`'s `MODE_SOURCE`; the emitter differs from §8.14's only in that key. `validation.rs` has no full-file pin, and the line map carries its basis keys. `w8_topology_model.py` pins topology.rs (`a82720cc…`) and `validate_components` (`b7c82ba4…`) at `97e6fda00f`. The line map carries every rule (869 remaps, no unmapped key). Its 32 pruned rules and two reviewed resolutions are §8.14's, identical to main's run. Rules added, by label:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **W8-CG** (`o10_rules.py`, the KD5C4 set) | `near_duplicate_nodes`' `.min(`/`.max(` receivers are the bounding box's f64 coordinates and an edge's usize node indices, and `scaled_norm`'s fold takes `m.max(x.abs())` over f64. The by-name scan also offered certified_publication's private FallbackError `min`/`max`, which changed KD5C6's entry cutset and stopped the run. The rule removes only those two callers' candidates, as for the other primitive callers. |
| **W8-L1** (`O10_LOOPS_LAST`) | The three `.take(NAMED)` maps (the described parts, the named coincident pairs and the named near pairs) are bounded at 6 each, each only in its own function. A guard (W8-NM) requires formation_guard's `NAMED` to be exactly 6. |
| **W8-L2** (`O10_LOOPS_LAST`) | `near_duplicate_nodes`' two passes `for (node, point) in coordinates.iter().enumerate()` (the grid fill and the 27-neighbour scan) visit each built node once, ≤ n. Text is reached from them only through the by-name false edges (`push`, `get`, `entry` to same-named methods elsewhere). Those conservative edges are kept and bounded rather than zeroed. The o10 check confirms that the header matched only `near_duplicate_nodes`, not the same header in `Bodies::new`. The scan's inner loops, including the pass over `found`, reach no text site that needs a further rule. |
| **W8-NI** (`O10_SITE_FROM`) | `node_id` is a closure passed as `&dyn Fn`, so the chain would charge its two alternative text sites once per call of `append_topology_diagnostics`. Per call it runs at most 60 times. In `disconnected_parts` that is NAMED for the description and NAMED for the refs (12). In `near_duplicate_nodes` it is two per named pair for each of the two messages and each of the two refs lists (4 × 2 × NAMED = 48). Both sites are charged at 60: the id clone (IN128) and `node#{index}` (5 + 20 B). The `more` closure is charged twice, once per message. **Guard:** the call-site counts of `node_id`, `refs` and `more` are read at the snapshot, and a change stops the run. |
| **W8-ID** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`) | These bounds apply to the composed texts at their sites. A part description is at most 22 literal bytes, plus the ordinal (20), a node id (128), the node count (20), `s` (1) and `no support` (10): 201 B. `described` is at most 6 · 201 + 5 · 2 = 1,216 B. The coincident `named` is at most 6 · (5 + 2 · 128) + 10 = 1,576 B, and the near `named` is at most 6 · (16 + 2 · 128 + 24) + 10 = 1,786 B, where `{distance:e}` is f64_exp, 24 B. `more` is at most 11 + 20 = 31 B. The generic `^more$|^named$` integer rule (lib.rs's counts) would price these Strings at 20 B, and the site audits replace it; `more(coincident_count)` and `more(near_count)` are audited the same way. The parts' and pairs' `node_id(..)` arguments are 128 B, the counts 20 B and the static suffixes their literal length. **Guard (W8-TX):** the templates are read at the snapshot, each once, and a changed one stops the run. |
| **W8-JN** (`LEX_SITE_SIZE`) | The three `.join("; ")` sites are bounded at their own sizes: 1,216, 1,576 and 1,786 B. Without this rule their receiver `()` matched formation_guard's fired-items join rule (50,176 B), an over-bound with another site's justification. |
| **W8-RF** (`O10_REFS_FIRST`, a new family in `lib/sq2_rules.py`) | The three warnings' affected refs. The parts' refs collect `.take(NAMED)` of the part map's keys, an exact-size iterator, so they hold at most 6 · (24 + 128) = 912 B. The pair refs push at most two ids per named pair and skip repeats, giving at most 12 Strings in a push-grown Vec of capacity 16: 16 · 24 + 12 · 128 = 1,920 B. |
| **W8-BT** (`O10_MULT0_SITES`, `O10_ID_AUDIT`) | D1 holds no components: `retained_memory.rs`'s admission refuses any non-empty `model.components` (`F::Components`, D1.4; P2). So `validate_components`' loop over `model.components` never runs on D1. The BRANCH_TOPOLOGY_INVALID warning and its id have multiplicity 0 on both routes, through the registered `model.components → 0` rule. The guard fails `o10_check` if either site's own multiplicity becomes positive. Its `degree` placeholder is audited as a usize (20 B). The node-degree map itself is built before the loop, so W8-ND prices it. |
| **W8-TW** (`w8_topology_model.py`, positive owner) | The working set of `append_topology_diagnostics`, once per ordinary run in O_base, every owner charged live at once with each grown owner's previous backing beside it. It drops before the boundary is prepared. At the caps (n = m = g = 32): the coordinates (3n f64); the element edges (push-grown, ≤ m: straight members, with no curved span or connector on D1 by P2/P3), with the replaced-span set charged as a table of m `&str` although it is empty on D1; a second Bodies (2n usize, the root map of ≤ n entries, 2 · 3n + n f64); the part map, a BTreeMap of ≤ m parts, since each part holds an element and parts are disjoint; it has at most one node per entry, each ≤ an internal node of 11 keys, 11 values, 12 edges, a parent pointer and two u16 (11 · (8 + 16) + 13 · 8 + 8 = 376 B); `first_of_body`, `with_element` (≤ m), `grounded` (≤ g) and `joined` (≤ m), each grown by insertion with its previous table; the cell grid of ≤ n cells, each slot a `[i64; 3]` key (3 · s(u64)) and a `Vec<usize>` header, with its previous table, and its cells' backings (Σ pushcap(k) ≤ 4n) plus one previous backing; the per-node `found` buffer of `(usize, Option<f64>)`, cleared per node with its capacity kept, holding that node's later neighbours in the 27 cells, each other node at most once because each node lies in one cell: ≤ n − 1 (capacity 32, previous 16); and the coincident `(usize, usize)` and near `(usize, usize, f64)` samples, each pushed only while shorter than NAMED (capacity 8, previous 4), with integer counts. No quadratic pair term remains. Three `Vec<String>` of ≤ NAMED headers complete it. The registered atoms s(f64) and s(u64) are new in O_base, and their in-build values are already collected. **Guard:** topology.rs's full-file sha256, the two validation bodies, each priced allocating statement (once), the single lib.rs call and topology.rs's inventory of owning constructs (nine `collect`, four `Vec::new()`, two `HashMap::new()`, one `HashSet::new()`, one `BTreeMap::new()`, one `Bodies::new(`, nine `.push(`, no `vec!`, `with_capacity`, `to_vec` or `Box::new`). A snapshot without topology.rs adds nothing; a partly present or changed owner stops the run. |
| **W8-ND** (`w8_topology_model.py`, positive owner) | `validate_components`' `HashMap<&str, usize>` of the pipe segments' end nodes holds ≤ 2m ids (two ends per segment; the ids are not yet checked against the nodes), grown by insertion, with its previous table, in O_base. |

### 8.17 Model document 0.7.0 actual-source D3 (W-13 S1b)

Model document 0.7.0 (DEL-02-01 Design [model-document-0.7.0.md](../../../PKG-02/DEL-02-01/Design/model-document-0.7.0.md) §2; owner OD-4, 2026-10-23) re-prices at `7222d954a8`, W-13 S1b merged on main `9bfd04c255`, so on the silent-zero guard's registration on W-8 (§8.15, "On W-8 (registration)"; W-8 itself is §8.16). It was first priced on the pre-0.4 unification source (`eac658ccfc`, coefficient-identical to §8.14) and then on W-8 alone (`a9c5d15293`, coefficient-identical to §8.16); the silent-zero guard registered first on W-8, and this D3 is re-derived on top of it. Against that registration's priced source (main `9bfd04c255`), the priced source differs only in version tokens, hash constants, comments, test modules and two diagnostic arguments; the changed lines in the priced crates are S1b's own against W-8, line for line:
- PP `model_format.rs`: `MODEL_SCHEMA_VERSION` 0.7.0 and `RETIRED_MODEL_SCHEMA_VERSIONS` gains 0.6.0 (a static array; no allocation);
- PP `pressure_runtime.rs`: the version token of two `validate_profile` literals (same length); `exact_admission.rs`, `retained_product.rs`, `source_recovery.rs` and `retained_memory.rs` (the admission prefix and `family_clauses`): comments only; `lib.rs`: test modules only;
- PP `retained_wire.rs` and RS `retained_precision.rs`: DEF-E's definition hash and the physics-retained-1 table hash; RS `semantic_contract.rs`: the pressure-2, pressure-source-1 and physics-retained-1 table hashes (`PRESSURE_TABLE_SHA256`, `PRESSURE_SOURCE_TABLE_SHA256`, `PHYSICS_RETAINED_SHA256`) and the version token of the v2 re-author text; RS `physics_source.rs` and `retained_precision.rs`: the current-version gates read 0.7.0 (same length);

- PP `case_state/resolve.rs` (`validate_document`) and `joint.rs`: the load/reference-state version-mismatch diagnostic and the joint authoring refusal format the shared `crate::MODEL_SCHEMA_VERSION` constant instead of a literal; the silent-zero guard's `resolve_case` and `refuse_all_reference_states` sites (SZ-LS) are main's, unchanged;

Nothing on the retained route calls the accessor, and the deserialized record is not a priced owner; the seven added `lib.rs` lines, all before line 320, move every snapshot-keyed `lib.rs` key and the carried `retained_w1` key (3619 → 3626) with unchanged text.

### 8.18 Absolute node-proximity tolerance actual-source D3 (REQ-04-06-012)

The near-duplicate node check's absolute, user-settable tolerance ([model-topology-checks.md](../../DEL-04-06/Design/model-topology-checks.md); REQ-04-06-012: 5 mm unless the user sets `model_settings.node_proximity_tolerance`) re-prices at `2b72ac3af7`, the commit that carries the pins and rules below. It merges main `8b4f584e7a` (model document 0.7.0, §8.17). It first priced at `f985fe297a` (product source `91f59b7a56`); Codex's round-1 review then moved the branch warning's statement of the applied factors after publication, and `2b72ac3af7` prices that source. The comparison basis is main's registered tree: main's committed tool prices `8b4f584e7a` with exit 0, and `generate --check` of that tree with §8.17's basis line reproduces §8.17's registered block with an empty diff. Against it the priced source moves in four files:
- PP `topology.rs`: `append_topology_diagnostics` reads `model.node_proximity_tolerance_m()` and runs `near_duplicate_nodes` only on `Ok`, passing the tolerance in metres. `near_duplicate_nodes` loses the bounding box (the `low`/`high` folds, the extent and `NEAR_DUPLICATE_RELATIVE`) and its `tolerance > 0.0` guard; its grid's cells are eight tolerances wide and indexed by `(x / width).floor() as i64` with no offset, so a far coordinate saturates; the 27-neighbour scan skips a neighbour whose index `checked_add` would overflow. NODE_NEAR_DUPLICATE's message names "the node-proximity tolerance ({tolerance:e} m)". The grid, the scan, the `found` buffer, the samples and the three warnings are otherwise W-8's (§8.16), now always run for two or more nodes;
- PP `validation.rs`: `validate_model_inputs` calls the new `validate_model_settings`, which pushes one blocking diagnostic with the accessor's code and message (refs: the setting's path) when the tolerance is malformed. BRANCH_TOPOLOGY_INVALID's message is now a multi-line `format!` with its topology text only. The new `state_applied_branch_factors(model_components, preview_cases, diagnostics)` appends to that warning the sides whose intensified rows the envelope publishes, read from the preview cases' `intensified_measures` records;
- PP `lib.rs`: `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner` calls `validation::state_applied_branch_factors(&model.components, &rendered.preview_cases, &mut envelope.diagnostics)` once. The call sits where the solved preview envelope takes its contract evidence, on the same line, so no lib.rs line moves;
- `model_settings.rs`: a doc comment only. The accessor and its five refusal texts are §8.17's later source (`755adbc180`); they are now reached from the retained route.

**Pins and carried rules.** Every changed span was read before re-pinning. `w8_topology_model.py` pins topology.rs (`a82720cc…` → `cca9abee…`, at `91f59b7a56`) and `validate_components` (`b7c82ba4…` → `3a242bc7…`); `branch_topology_invalid` is unchanged. W8-ND's node-degree map is unchanged in `validate_components`. Two priced statements move by indentation only (the coincident and near sample pushes, one block shallower without the `tolerance > 0.0` guard). topology.rs's inventory of owning constructs is unchanged (nine `collect`, four `Vec::new()`, two `HashMap::new()`, one `HashSet::new()`, one `BTreeMap::new()`, one `Bodies::new(`, nine `.push(`): the removed bounding box held only stack arrays, and `checked_add` allocates nothing. lib.rs's full-file pin moves (`0380ae97…` → `2e95bd8f…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py` (SOURCE_FILES), `w1_reductions_model.py` (REDUCED_PINS) and `kd5_text_model.py` (the KD5C4 primitive pin). Each model's owners were re-read against the one changed line: it adds one call and no `.min(`/`.max(` call, and it changes no owner or lifetime those models price. Because no line moves, no snapshot-keyed lib.rs key moves either. The line map carries every rule (869 remaps, no unmapped key). Its 32 pruned rules and two reviewed resolutions are main's, entry for entry, and none lies in a file this change touches, so no bound is dropped against the registered tree. Rules changed or added, by label:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **W8-CG** (re-argued) | `near_duplicate_nodes`' remaining `.min(`/`.max(` receivers are an edge's usize node indices (`a.min(b)`, `a.max(b)`); the bounding box's f64 folds are gone. `scaled_norm`'s fold still takes `m.max(x.abs())` over f64. The same two callers' by-name candidates are removed. |
| **W8-L1, W8-L2, W8-TW** (re-argued, formulas unchanged) | The `.take(NAMED)` maps and the two passes over the built nodes keep their exact headers and bounds (≤ 6 and ≤ n). The grid still holds ≤ n cells, each node in exactly one: saturation only merges far nodes into a shared cell (fewer cells, the same n entries, Σ pushcap ≤ 4n). In the scan, distinct offsets give distinct keys when `checked_add` succeeds and none when it fails, so each cell is visited at most once per node and each other node enters `found` at most once: ≤ n − 1, as before. A user's larger tolerance can only make more pairs qualify; the samples stay at NAMED with integer counts. No quadratic term appears, and W8-TW's charge (32,370 B at native atoms) is unchanged. |
| **W8-ID, W8-TX** (re-keyed) | The near message's site is re-keyed to the new template (`"{} pair{} of distinct nodes lie no farther apart than`). Its placeholders keep their bounds: the count (20), the plural (20), `{tolerance:e}` by the inherited LowerExp class (24, as before), `named` 1,786 and `more` 31. Its literal grows from 158 to 161 B, so the site is 2,042 B. The other templates the guard reads are unchanged. |
| **W8-BT** (re-keyed) | The warning's message is now a multi-line `format!`, so the `degree` audit (a usize, 20 B) is keyed to its `format!(` row. `component.node` keeps the inherited identifier class (IN128). The multiplicity-0 guards on the warning and its id are unchanged. |
| **W8-BF** (`O10_LOOPS_FIRST`, `O10_MULT0_SITES`, `O10_ID_AUDIT`, `LEX_SITE_SIZE`) | `state_applied_branch_factors` is called once per run, from the solved preview envelope, with `&model.components`. Everything it allocates lies inside its loop `for component in model_components`. D1 refuses any component (P2, D1.4: `F::Components`), so that loop is bound 0 on both routes. It is a U6-LF rule on its own exact header, matching only this function: the route-E rule for `for component in components` (bound 6, `physics_evidence.rs`) would otherwise capture a shared header. All the function's sites therefore have multiplicity 0, and the side text and the statement are guarded by `o10_check`. Its texts are still bounded at their sites. A side `{side} SIF to {pipe}` is a static side ("header" or "branch", 6 B) plus a published record's pipe id (an admitted pipe id, IN128): 142 B. At most two sides are named: one per role, because a role's records name the one pipe that role's reference names, and they are deduplicated. Joined by ", " they are at most 286 B (the join's lexicon site and the statement's `sides` placeholder). The statement's literal adds 48 B, so the `push_str` site is 334 B. **Guard:** the stop condition reads the function at the snapshot: its signature, then the one loop, which closes the body, so nothing allocates outside it. It also reads the two-role loop, the two templates, the one join, `Vec::new()`, push and `push_str`, the one call in `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner`, and no other caller in lib.rs or validation.rs. A change stops the run. |
| **NP-MS** (positive) | **Premise check.** D1 can carry a malformed tolerance. No D1.3–D1.8 clause (`family_clauses`) names `model_settings`, and the typed census does not read it; the raw census bounds its unit as raw text (RawTextBytes, l ≤ 128) and its control bytes. A finite JSON value ≤ 0, a unit outside the catalog's lengths, or a value whose conversion to metres overflows or underflows deserializes and reaches `validate_model_settings`. No zero has a premise, so the site keeps the chain's own multiplicity: once per `validate_model_inputs` call (2 at E, 1 in the X variant). The run then returns its blocked envelope before the build, so the topology call meets only `Ok`; the chain still charges the accessor's texts there too. **Bounds.** The accessor returns at most one refusal per call, but the chain charges each of its five texts at every call (3 at E), an over-bound kept. The five are 424, 418, 218, 263 and 275 B: the path constant at its literal bytes (NP-PT), the authored unit IN128 (it was identifier-unaudited), and the value in Display (327) or Debug (24) by the inherited f64 classes. The diagnostic's message `error.message` moves one of those texts, at most 39 + 58 + 327 = 424 B. The chain prices a moved non-literal message by its argument class, here the inherited `error.message` class (8,192 B); a site-scoped diagnostic-message bound would need a new chain mechanism, and the over-bound is 8,192 − 424 = 7,768 B per refusal wherever the message is counted. The refs are the one path String (the default `vec!` element, 24 + 128 B); its `to_string` site is NP-PT's 39 B. The units lookup reached through the accessor (`unit_by_symbol`) keeps its inherited pricing, now with three more calls at E (+13,038 B requested). **Guard:** the path literal, the five templates (each once in its function, five `format!(` in the production file), the diagnostic's one `diag(` with its id, code, severity, message and refs, and the single call from `validate_model_inputs` are read at the snapshot; a change stops the run. §8.26 (SM-MS) extends the guard to the shear-model refusal. |
| **NP-PT** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`) | `NODE_PROXIMITY_TOLERANCE_PATH` is one plain `&'static str` literal in `model_settings.rs`, read at the snapshot (39 B). It is audited at those bytes in the five texts and at the refs' `to_string`, instead of the inherited constant class's 512 B. |

The run at `f985fe297a` completes on all four points with no unmapped header or unclassified argument, and both o10 checks are at zero. Its tree is identical to the run at `91f59b7a56` with the same tool (`identical_but_basis`). `compare` against main's tree gives `a_le_b` false and `a_ge_b` true, with the same structure. 1,478 coefficients are equal and 98 rise: 92 form coefficients with their `success__` copies, and six text atoms.
- **TAV.** `TAV_W[1]` 4,783,686,794 → 4,783,750,542 (+63,748 B): the five accessor texts at three calls (9,588), the refusal diagnostic at two calls (40,960), its refs' `to_string` (156), the units lookup's three calls (13,038) and the near message (+6). `TAV_X[1]` 5,366,521,926 → 5,366,557,574 (+35,648 B), the same terms at the X variant's two accessor calls and one validation call. `TAV_text_requested` and `TAV_text_moving` rise by 63,748 B. Route L's points rise by 72,152 B, the extra 8,404 B from the precommit reader's loops over two more diagnostics (`preview_physics_evidence.rs`, the D fixpoint), as in §8.16.

### 8.19 Silent-drop guard actual-source D3

The pressure-free route's input closure ([pressure-contract.md](../../DEL-04-07/Design/pressure-contract.md), "Pressure-free input closure"; Agent 0, 2026-10-23) re-prices at `43856d7964`, the commit that carries the pins and rules below. It merges main `f1da661c6d`, so it is priced on the absolute node-proximity tolerance's registration (§8.18). The comparison basis is main's registered tree: main's committed tool prices `f1da661c6d` with exit 0, and `generate --check` of that tree with §8.18's basis line reproduces §8.18's registered block with an empty diff. Against it the priced source moves in four files:
- PP `lib.rs`: `mod model_fields;`; the wire records close (`deny_unknown_fields` on `PreviewModelWire`, `PreviewLoadCase`, `PreviewPrimitiveLoad`, `PreviewCombination`, its terms, the load target and the equivalent-static inputs), with declared, solve-ignored `IgnoredAny` members (the root's `data_boundary`, `diagnostics` and model document 0.7.0's five record lists; a load case's `kind`, `label`, `status`, `tags`, `notes`; a combination's `tags`, `notes`) and closed quantities; and one call, `model_fields::unknown_field_refusal(&actual_request)`, first in `run_linear_static_preview_value_dispatch`, returning `Err` on any finding. Every snapshot-keyed `lib.rs` line moves +59 before that call and +63 after it, with unchanged text;
- PP `model_fields.rs` (new): the scan of the actual request's closed records (`check`, `named`, `objects`) and the refusal text; the accepted key lists are `'static` slices read from the serde derives;
- PP `preview_physics.rs`: `append_combination_results` reports a row missing in the first operand as LOAD_COMBINATION_SOURCE_RESULT_MISSING (one closure for both sites), names every withheld row in `withheld` and pushes one LOAD_COMBINATION_ROW_WITHHELD warning per combination naming at most eight, and builds each support's group in a `rows` Vec that publishes whole or not at all;
- PP `validation.rs`: `validate_mechanics_combination` keeps a `seen` set of the terms' case ids and pushes the blocking LOAD_COMBINATION_DUPLICATE_TERM once per repeated case, at its second occurrence (independent review, round 1: a case repeated three times had produced two diagnostics with one id); `validate_range_envelope_combination`'s existing duplicate-operand diagnostic follows the same rule. Both loops now enumerate, and each counts a repeated id's earlier occurrences in the slice before it (`[..index].iter().filter(..).count()`), with no allocation.

**Pins and carried rules.** Every changed span was read before re-pinning. lib.rs's full-file pin moves (`2e95bd8f…` → `86bc015f…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py` (SOURCE_FILES), `w1_reductions_model.py` (REDUCED_PINS) and `kd5_text_model.py` (the KD5C4 primitive pin); preview_physics.rs's (`a8330f13…` → `a906bf72…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py` and `w1_reductions_model.py`. Each model's owners were re-read against the changes: no `.min(`/`.max(` call is added or removed; the L5 guard, shadow, insertion and call texts are unchanged (`w1_reductions_model.guard` passes); and the callee keeps its inputs (the sorted `base_ids` and cloned `case_rows`), so no reduction's owner or lifetime moves. The snapshot keys move with unchanged text: `b7a_domain_model.py`'s nineteen audit keys, `b7a_owner_choices.py`'s helpers (14056/14057/14477/14478 → 14119/14120/14540/14541), `b7a_phase_model.py`'s `SOLVE` (4782 → 4845) and `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner` (2642 → 2705), `b7a_returning_errors.py`'s owners, sites and callers, and `b7a_scoped_text_model.py`'s four lines; the tool's tests follow them. The `_X` emitter, with `retained_w1` carried to `lib.rs:3689` (its only difference from §8.17's `b8ad3656…`), adds hash `836d05df…` to `MODE_SOURCE`. The line map carries every rule (869 remaps, no unmapped key); its 32 pruned rules and two reviewed resolutions are main's, entry for entry. Rules changed or added, by label:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **SD-RK** (`sq2_decisions_common.py`, re-keyed by name) | Two basis keys of `append_combination_results`' audited sites no longer carry by line: the source-missing diagnostic's refs (basis `preview_physics.rs:990`), now in the `source_missing` closure, whose text the new warning's refs repeat, and a support component row's source refs (basis `:1022`), now pushed to the group's `rows`. Both are resolved at the snapshot to the same sites, with unchanged audits (IN128, RES). A source without the closure keeps the basis keys. |
| **SD-NF** (`O10_SITE_ZERO`; guard) | **Premise.** On a permitted call the scan finds nothing. The scan is the value entry's first statement; on any finding it returns `Err` before `CapturedInvocation::parse` and before `retained_memory::admit`, so a request with an undeclared key is never captured or admitted. The finding push in `check` and the refusal text after the scan's one early return (the per-finding `format!`, the `join`, the `and N more` suffix and the refusal `format!`) therefore have multiplicity 0. **Guard:** the call's text and place (first in the dispatch, before the parse and the admission), its single caller in `lib.rs` and in `model_fields.rs`, the one `.push(` in `model_fields.rs` and the one early return. A change stops the run. |
| **SD-L1** (`O10_LOOPS_LAST`) | The scan's loops, each only in its own function: the authored load cases (`cases`), one case's primitive loads (`l`), the combinations (`z`) and one combination's terms (3, D1.4), each visited once; `check`'s key loop visits one closed record's keys, all declared on a permitted call (SD-NF), so at most the largest closed record's declared members, the model root's 23, counted at the snapshot from `PreviewModelWire` (the run stops if another closed record is larger). The wind exposed-span loop keeps the inherited D1.5 rule (0: no equivalent-static input). |
| **SD-ID** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`, `LEX_SITE_SIZE`) | The scan's composed names and paths at their sites. A record name is `{kind} `{id}`` (a static kind of at most 14 B, the record's raw id IN128) or `{kind} {index}` (a usize): a load case's at most 140 B, a primitive load's 145 B and with ` in ` and its case's 289 B, a combination's 142 B. A path is a static prefix with usize indices: `model.load_cases[{i}]` 38 B, its `.equivalent_static` 56 B, a load's 76 B, `model.combinations[{i}]` 40 B. The per-record closures that would name a finding (`|| …`) are charged at their sites' chain multiplicity, an over-bound kept. The zeroed refusal text is still bounded: a finding's field IN128, its record at most 306 B, its path at most 97 B, one finding 552 B, at most eight joined. **Guard:** the three record kinds and the names' and paths' statements are read at the snapshot; a change stops the run. |
| **SD-EV** (`O10_EDGE_ZERO`) | `findings.push(..)` in `check` is `Vec::push`; by name the call graph also reaches `load_ledger::LoadLedger::push`, which `check` never calls. |
| **SD-WT** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`, `LEX_SITE_SIZE`) | The withheld names keep their chain multiplicities (per base id, per support, per combination). A base-id name is a result base id (RES, 1,024 B) with a static reason, at most 1,052 B; a support's is `support-action:{len}:{id} ({reason})` with the group's longer static reason (45 B), 212 B. The warning names at most eight joined by `, ` (8,430 B) plus `, and N more`; its id is `identity(&[&combination.id])` (COMP) and its refs one combination id. **Guard:** the three pushes, the two reasons, `NAMED` = 8 and the one `take(NAMED)` join are read at the snapshot. |
| **SD-DT** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`) | The duplicate-term diagnostic's id (two `stable_suffix` ids) and refs (two id clones) are IN128, as its sibling per-term diagnostics. It fires at most once per repeated case; the chain keeps the site's full multiplicity, once per term per call, an over-bound kept. D1.4 does not refuse a repeated term, so the diagnostic is reachable on route L with combinations. |
| **SD-DL** (`O10_LOOPS_LAST`) | The two duplicate loops' headers changed to `for (index, term) in combination.terms.iter().enumerate()` (`validate_mechanics_combination`) and `for (index, operand_id) in operand_ids.iter().enumerate()` (`validate_range_envelope_combination`), so the inherited rules for the former headers (`for term in &combination.terms`, `for operand_id in operand_ids`, both 3) no longer match them. Each visits one combination's terms or range operands once: at most 3 (D1.4), each rule matching only its own function. The earlier-occurrence count inside each is a borrowing `.iter().filter(..).count()` over at most two earlier entries: it allocates nothing and reaches no text site, so the chain registers no loop for it. **Guard:** each header and its complete earlier-occurrence statement (line breaks removed: the `[..index]` slice, the `.iter().filter(..).count()` chain and the predicate) are read at the snapshot, with the function's one `.count()` and one `[..index]` and no `.to_vec()`, `.collect`, `.cloned()` or `.to_owned()`; the SD owners model pins the same two statements. A change stops the run (exit 4). (Independent review, round 2: a guard on the chain's tail alone let a changed slice or an added allocation pass.) |
| **SD-WH** (`sd_owners_model.py`, positive owner) | Per combination, one at a time, in the combination callee beside the W1-L5 index (route L with z ≥ 1, `AggregationSuffix`): `withheld`, a push-grown `Vec<String>` of at most one name per base id and one per support (≤ PA + g), with its previous backing (the names' text is TEXT, SD-WT); the warning's `.take(NAMED).cloned()` list, eight `String`s of at most 1,052 B each, which `.join` consumes; and one support group's `rows`, a push-grown `Vec<ResultItem>` of at most eight rows with its previous backing, moved into `results`. **Guard:** the statements, each once in its function, and the group's two pushes; a partly present or changed owner stops the run. |
| **SD-HS, SD-PB** (`sd_owners_model.py`, positive owners) | `validate_mechanics_combination`'s `seen`, a `HashSet<&str>` of a combination's at most three term ids, grown by insertion with its previous table, transient within each call (`BuildScratch`); and the scan's probe error, the boxed `field probe` text (11 B) that each `declared_fields` probe returns and drops at once (one at a time). |

The run at `bc99d4d09f` (before the review's duplicate rule) completes on all four points with no unmapped header or unclassified argument, and both o10 checks are at zero. `compare` against main's tree gives `a_le_b` false and `a_ge_b` true, with the same structure. 1,562 coefficients are equal and 14 rise: `TAV_W[1]` and `TAV_X[1]` 4,783,750,542 → 4,784,996,136 and 5,366,557,574 → 5,367,803,168 (+1,245,594 B each, the scan's names and paths at E's and L(3,0)'s caps: 3 cases and 384 primitive loads), `TAV_text_requested` and `TAV_text_moving` by the same, `O_base_*[1]` +49 B and `O_base_*[s(&str)]` +4 (SD-HS, SD-PB), with their `success__` copies. D (76,386 at E), D_env 28,806 and Text(diag_env) 269,528,816 are unchanged: route E and L(3,0) have no combinations. Route L's points with combinations rise further (L(2,1) TAV +11,843,910 B, D +7; L(1,2) TAV +22,442,226 B, D +14), from the withheld names, the duplicate-term diagnostics and the per-combination warning, and SD-WH adds to their aggregation; they stay below the merged tree.

At native atoms every phase rises by 1,245,707 B in both modes; W3 is still the maximum. P_FINAL (2,595) and P_FINAL_EXACT (3,747) are unchanged. ### 8.20 Member end actions in global coordinates actual-source D3 (W-7)

W-7's member end actions in global coordinates ([global-end-actions.md](../../DEL-04-01/Design/global-end-actions.md); PRD FR-SOL-007; Agent 0, option A, 2026-10-23, confirmed 2026-10-24) re-price at `8afaa282b3`, the commit that carries the pins, rules and bounds below. It merges main `2653f87392`, so it is priced on the silent-drop guard's registration (§8.19). The comparison basis is main's registered tree: main's committed tool prices `2653f87392` with exit 0, and `generate --check` of that tree with §8.19's basis line reproduces §8.19's registered block with an empty diff. Against it the priced source moves in these files:
- PP `lib.rs`: `solve_prepared_load_case` records each member's first row position before its element-local end rows and, on every publication but a source-recovery one (`global_end_rows_published`), appends its twelve global end rows (`append_element_global_end_results`, F = Rᵀ f per end from the published local rows), or one blocking ELEMENT_FORCE_RECOVERY_FAILED diagnostic when the frame or a rotated value is unavailable; `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner` drops them in place (`retain`) on a source-selected publication, and `qualify_source_case_rows` filters them out before binding. Every snapshot-keyed `lib.rs` line moves +5 after the aggregation, +8 after the member's row position, +37 after the global end block and +156 after the new helper and its constants, with unchanged text;
- PP `retained_product.rs`: the binder binds a global end row as a recipe-free not-covered row; `failed_manifest` adds each member's twelve placeholders after its maximum (`FAILED_MEMBER_ROWS` 66 → 78); `prepared_not_covered` reserves a per-member end-action table and completes each global end row from the projected end actions (`global_end_value`), on case and, now, combination freezes; `validate_final_metadata` gains the global end arm;
- PP `formation_guard.rs`: the stress-free notice's sentence names the element-local member rows and the support rows (a longer static literal);
- PP `preview_physics.rs`: the two kinds join `LINEAR_STATE_KINDS` (a static array);
- FK `final_case.rs`: `row_scales_reduced` admits a not-covered force or moment row in a combination (a predicate);
- RE `global_end_evidence.rs` (new; the readers' row semantics and coverage), `physics_evidence.rs` and `preview_physics_evidence.rs` (one call each), `retained_precision.rs` (the not-covered classification) and `semantic_contract.rs` (the table digests).

**The planned bound change.** The ordinary envelope carries the global end rows on every run, so the priced rows per member rise by 12 on both routes: P_FINAL = 7n + 78m + 8g + 3 = 2,979 and P_FINAL_EXACT = 7n + 114m + 8g + 3 = 4,131 at the caps (n = m = g = 32), in `retained_memory.rs` and in the tool's models (W7-RL, W7-RP). The bounds that lean on the row counts are re-established:
- **Route-E row text.** Route L's 2·C_eq·P_final·Text(row) still bounds route E's rows: 7n + 114m + 8g + 3 ≤ 2·(7n + 78m + 8g + 3) is 0 ≤ 7n + 42m + 8g + 3, true for all n, m, g ≥ 0; at the caps 4,131 ≤ 5,958 per result set and 12,393 ≤ 17,874 at C_eq = 3. Text(row) stays 11,474 B.
- **Capacities.** Route L's C_eq·P_final is 3 · 2,979 = 8,937 rows, above the former 8,192 slots, so its push capacity is 16,384; route E's 3 · 4,131 = 12,393 rows stay within its 16,384 (U6-RB-V). The chain prices both from its own P (the typed patches).
- **G-C.** `phase_caps_on`: route L 8,937 rows and 16,384 slots (was 7,785 and 8,192), route E 12,393 and 16,384 (was 11,241). The cap-maximal exact input observes 12,387 rows, inside route E's 12,393 and above route L's 8,937 (`witness_tests::f1_g_c_…`).
- **FAILED_MEMBER_ROWS** 66 → 78: U4B-RL's guard admits 51, 66 or 78 (W7-RL); route E's census guard still requires 102 rows per member on the pressure-source-1 fixtures (U6-RP, U4B-RP) and adds the twelve the census omits (W7-RP).

### 8.22 Component weight and centre of gravity as nodal weight loads actual-source D3 (W-15 slice b)

W-15 slice b ([rigid-component-stiffness-scaling.md](../../../PKG-03/DEL-03-05/Design/rigid-component-stiffness-scaling.md) §3; the owner's R10 ruling, option A) re-prices at `600a04436d`, the commit that carries the pins and rules below. It sits on main `f02e915528`, so it is priced on W-15 a+d's registration (§8.21). The comparison basis is main's registered tree: main's committed tool prices `f02e915528` with exit 0, and `generate --check` of that tree with §8.21's basis line reproduces §8.21's registered block with an empty diff. Against the basis the priced source moves in four files:
- PP `self_weight.rs`: the generator applies an eligible rigid-family component's weight as one nodal force and up to three nodal moments (`component_weights`, `component_primitive`, `wrench`, `component_load_id`), and `inspect_applied_self_weight` checks their lineage (`generation_basis`, `component_basis`) and staleness. Its node arm of `deterministic_identity` is now `load.id.starts_with("load:self-weight:")` (`self_weight.rs:1289`, before: `false`), and the method test recognizes `component_weight_at_center_of_gravity…` (1267-1277);
- primitive_loads `lib.rs`: `category_allows_node_target` admits `Weight` (2521-2524). `prepare_node_load` (2371-2411) still pushes exactly one `LoadFinding` (the `UnsupportedTargetForCategory` refusal at 2388, or the node or dimension refusals) or one `NodalLoadContribution` (2406) per load, so a nodal weight load moves from the finding push to the contribution push. Every D1 load was already priced at the larger of the two, since D1 admits nodal wind, seismic and occasional loads;
- PP `rigid_component.rs`: `mapping_conflicts` (186) wraps `conflicts` for `component_weights` (`self_weight.rs:560`), which is reached only from the zeroed `generate_operations` (`self_weight.rs:294`). W15-PM's two live member maps stand on D1;
- PP `retained_memory.rs`: `family_clauses`' node arm refuses a node load whose id starts with `load:self-weight:` (`retained_memory.rs:986-988`, W15B-NS below).

No reader, FK or result_export file changes. The retained route still refuses every component (P2).

**Narrowing (W15B-NS).** The 17 downstream self-weight function zeros (§8.2, `b7a_formation_model`) and the site zeros after `inspect_applied_self_weight`'s `continue` rest on one premise: every admitted load takes the `continue` at `if !recognized && !looks_generated` (`self_weight.rs:1294-1296`). D1.10 refuses an object provenance, so `method` is None and `recognized` and `claimed_method_family` are false. Before slice b, `deterministic_identity` was false for a node target, and B7a (b) refused the element form by `self_weight_form` (`GeneratedLoadIdentity`). Slice b's node arm makes a node load in the generated namespace reach the lineage closure on D1. The premise is restored by enforcement, as the element form's was: D1.7's node arm refuses that load by the same fact, with exactly the product's test (`starts_with("load:self-weight:")`). T4-U6/U8's two `family_clauses` refusals (§8.9) are the precedent for a D3 narrowing of admission. The refusal loses no successful solve. Outside D1, such a load without a lineage record the inspection can derive is blocking: `SELF_WEIGHT_INPUT_INVALID` (`self_weight.rs:63-72`, from the lineage closure's `fail`), or `PROVENANCE_INPUT_MISSING` when it has no provenance, with `MODEL_INCOMPLETE` and no result. A load with a lineage record carries object provenance, which D1.10 already refused; while its component is present, P2 refuses that too. D1.10 does not depend on the component: a load wrapped in valid `MANUAL_OVERRIDE_METHOD` preservation records after the components are cleared is inspected as `ManualOverride` (`self_weight.rs:1312`, 1442) and solves on the ordinary route with a warning, but its object provenance kept it outside D1 before and after this narrowing. `retained_facade_tests::w15b_generated_namespace_node_loads_block_and_are_refused_by_d1` checks this on PF and EX in both modes, over three namespace ids, seven non-object provenances, three object provenances and four payloads. The ordinary route blocks and has no result. The retained route refuses at G-A by `GeneratedLoadIdentity`, runs no W1 and publishes exactly the ordinary route's bytes, so the user sees the blocking diagnostic naming the load and its case; nothing is dropped. With `ef1df51dfd`'s admission the same matrix was admitted (non-object provenance, W1 ran) or refused by `ObjectProvenance`, and every run published the ordinary route's blocked bytes with no successor. `w15b_hand_written_nodal_weight_stays_admitted` and the law test keep a nodal `weight` load under any other id inside D1.7.

**The readers need no mirror.** No law or test binds `family_clauses` to the readers, and the readers do not re-check D1's refusals of authored loads. They do not check D1.10's provenance. They read a node load's `force` or `moment` dimension only while re-deriving the nodal terms (`retained_precision.rs:5447-5451`, and `tests/verifiers/retained_precision.py` at 2387), not as an admission refusal. The element-form mirror exists for a different reason. The receipt's `member_load_terms` must equal exactly the set the producer's W1 member capture forms from the authored element loads. So the precommit reader's G8 (`retained_precision.rs` `verify_member_terms`, and `tests/verifiers/retained_precision.py` at 2098) re-derives which element loads become member terms, and the producer excludes the deterministic self-weight form there. Nodal terms are re-derived from every authored node load by the unchanged nodal rules, whatever its id. A receipt with a node load in the generated namespace cannot be produced, because G-A refuses before any capture. So no reader set or derivation depends on this refusal.

**Pins and carried rules.** Every changed span was read before re-pinning. Full-file pins: primitive_loads `lib.rs` (`c8a3571d…` → `22731163…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py`; `self_weight.rs` (`a1e523fd…` → `d28d66b4…`) in `b7a_formation_model.py`; `rigid_component.rs` (`be1425b5…` → `0203c6aa…`) in `w15_owners_model.py` (W15-PM's statements unchanged). Admission pins: `family_clauses` (`e234ac88…` → `fe83ef92…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py` and `batch5_model.py`, and the admission prefix (`b3d21347…` → `930e099f…`); the caps, census and late admission bodies are byte-identical. The element identity audit's snapshot key moves `self_weight.rs` 869 → 1281 with unchanged text, and the 17 zeros' warrant names the node refusal. The line map carries every rule (877 remaps, no unmapped key); its 32 pruned rules and two reviewed resolutions are main's, entry for entry. Rules added:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **W15B-NS** (`O10_EDGE_ZERO`, `O10_SITE_ZERO`, guarded) | The edge `inspect_applied_self_weight` → `generation_basis` is zero: its two calls (`self_weight.rs:1320`, 1325) are inside the lineage closure after the `continue` every admitted load takes (above). `generation_basis`' callees `component_basis`, `normalized_weight`, `normalized_cog` and `component_load_id` are otherwise reached only from the zeroed `generate_operations`. The component branch's wrench-set key closure (1380, two `to_string`s per row) runs only inside the same closure. **Guard:** the node arm's prefix test, the `continue`, `looks_generated`'s definition, the two `generation_basis` calls in the function (three in the file) and both `GeneratedLoadIdentity` refusals in `family_clauses`, read at the snapshot; `tests/test_w15b_component_weight.py` mutates each. |

The runs at the worktree state and at `600a04436d` complete on all four points with exit 0, no unmapped header or unclassified argument and both o10 checks at zero. Each of the five trees is byte-identical to main's at `f02e915528`, and `compare` against main's tree gives `identical_but_basis`: all 1,586 coefficients are equal. D (L(3,0) 43,525, L(2,1) 39,240, L(1,2) 35,235, E 76,482), D_env (28,902), TAV_W (5,065,376,254 B), TAV_X (5,517,690,586 B), Text(diag_env) (270,642,448 B), Text(row) (11,474 B), P_FINAL (78 rows per member) and P_FINAL_EXACT (114) are unchanged. Without W15B-NS the generator's lineage functions price positively, with D at L(3,0) 47,365 and E 80,322, and the run is incomplete (exit 5) with unclassified arguments: 15 argument groups, 26 occurrences, at every point, in the independent review's control run.

**Registration.** The generated block's basis is "SQ2 G5 at 600a04436d: the maximum over routes L (C_eq <= 3: worst shape c = 3, z = 0) and E (c = 3), SQ2 rules; text at l <= 128". It has 324 atoms, 100 forms, 26 SourceUpper and no Estimate (1,586 coefficients), and PHASES = 8; `generate --check` reproduces it. No law-test or challenge pin moves. All 80 native evaluations fit at the unchanged policy, with every phase equal to §8.21's. ### 8.23 Imposed displacement on the pressure-free route actual-source D3 (W-16 slice a)

W-16 slice a ([pf-imposed-displacement.md](../../DEL-04-03/Design/pf-imposed-displacement.md) §7; the owner's ruling, 2026-10-23, "Row 2 I approve option A") re-prices at `6d4e0c1506`, the commit that carries the pins, rules and owners below. It merges main `947b323d03`, so it is priced on W-15 slice b's registration (§8.22). The comparison basis is main's registered tree: main's committed tool prices `947b323d03` with exit 0, and `generate --check` of that tree with §8.22's basis line reproduces §8.22's registered block with an empty diff. The design states no change to P_FINAL, and none occurs. Against the basis the priced source moves in these files:
- PP `lib.rs`: `mod imposed_motion;`; `LoadTargetInput` gains `Support { support, dof }`; the value dispatch drops its call of the pre-parse imposed-displacement refusal; `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner` calls `imposed_motion::validate` after the profile and document checks and returns the blocked envelope on a blocking finding; `solve_prepared_load_case` forms a case's prescribed map when it has no load state (`imposed_motion::case_prescribed`), returns an empty `LoadCaseSolve` on a blocking finding pushed there, and reads that map into the prescribed values and the structural solve; `build_load_case_primitive_loads` filters support targets out of the forces (its match's new support arm is `None`). Every snapshot-keyed `lib.rs` line moves +1, +5, +2, +7, +31 to +35 or +37 to +39 with unchanged text;
- PP `imposed_motion.rs` (new): `validate` (the exact route's refusal of every support-targeted primitive; on the pressure-free route `validate_pressure_free`'s named checks), `case_prescribed`, `refuse` and the rigid-support predicates;
- PP `preview_physics.rs`: `LIMITATIONS[1]` drops "and so is a support displacement imposed outside a load-state model" (69 B shorter), and `imposed_displacement_refusal` is deleted;
- PP `model_fields.rs`: `target_fields`' support arm (a `'static` slice);
- PP `retained_memory.rs`: the census walk's support arm counts its two strings, and `family_clauses`' D1.7 refuses the support target on both routes (`F::LoadTarget`);
- PP `retained_product.rs`: the captured-target match's support arm (`false`); PP `self_weight.rs`: `primitive_payload`'s support arm and `inspect_applied_self_weight`'s (`false`);
- RE `retained_precision.rs` (`TABLE_HASH`) and `semantic_contract.rs` (the two preview table digests), literals only; both preview tables (slot 1).

No FK, primitive_loads or other reader file changes. The retained route refuses every support-targeted primitive at D1.7, so no admitted case carries motion.

**What D1 reaches.** D1.7's node arm checks only the dimension (`force` or `moment`), not the category, so a node-targeted primitive with category `imposed_displacement` and a force or moment dimension is admitted. On route L, `validate_pressure_free` refuses it by name (`IMPOSED_DISPLACEMENT_TARGET_INVALID`, then `continue`), at most once per primitive load; before W-16 the same load failed `parse_category` in generic validation (already priced). On route E, `validate` takes the exact branch, which iterates only support targets. `case_prescribed` runs for every case solved without a load state, on both routes (route E's `load_state` is None by B7b-LS), and builds its node index before its loop.

**Pins and carried rules.** Every changed span was read before re-pinning. Full-file pins: lib.rs (`fb94f699…` → `8b437bcc…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py`, `w1_reductions_model.py` and `kd5_text_model.py` (the KD5C4 primitive pin: no `.min(`/`.max(` call added); preview_physics.rs (`b56fdb63…` → `5b1dc6f0…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py` and `w1_reductions_model.py`; retained_product.rs (`10a045d7…` → `11052a7b…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py` and `w1_member_load_model.py`; self_weight.rs (`d28d66b4…` → `da644546…`) in `b7a_formation_model.py`; RE retained_precision.rs (`8fab9d0f…` → `46f4163f…`) in `w1_member_load_model.py` and `b7a_domain_model.py`, with RE semantic_contract.rs (`99389cff…` → `c877df1d…`). Admission pins: `family_clauses` (`fe83ef92…` → `2e23cc04…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py` and `batch5_model.py`, and the admission prefix (`930e099f…` → `1ede7561…`), whose only changes are the census's support arm and D1.7's refusal; the caps and late admission bodies are byte-identical. Body pins: `solve_prepared_load_case` in `kd5_text_model.py`, `kd5_first_error_model.py` and `batch5_model.py` (`f7799193…` → `2544b007…`) and in `o4_owners_model.py` (`27d888b1…` → `e711a993…`); its added statements add no literal, label, record constructor, route string, carrier, staging, fallback attempt or first-return alternative. The failed Preview manifest's formulation allowance, read from the current literals, falls 3,138 → 3,069 B (the sentence). The snapshot keys move with unchanged text: `b7a_domain_model.py`'s audit keys (lib.rs +5 to +39, retained_product.rs +2), `b7a_owner_choices.py`'s helpers (14386/14387/14807/14808 → 14425/14426/14846/14847) and `freeze_combination` (7392 → 7394), `b7a_phase_model.py`'s `SOLVE` (4910 → 4917) and finalizer caller (2750 → 2752), `b7a_returning_errors.py`'s owners, sites and callers, `b7a_scoped_text_model.py`'s four lib.rs lines (+35, +39) and `b7a_formation_model.py`'s element identity audit (self_weight.rs 1281 → 1284); the `_X` emitter, with `retained_w1` carried to `lib.rs:3752` (its only difference from §8.21's `3f1e9c51…`), adds hash `59bffb88…` to `MODE_SOURCE`. The line map carries every rule (872 remaps, no unmapped key). It prunes 37 rules: main's 32, entry for entry (three callers' targets moved +2), and five ID-audit keys of the deleted `imposed_displacement_refusal` (basis `preview_physics.rs:1093`-`1095`, `1119`, `1122`), each by a reviewed resolution in `rules/linemap_resolutions.json` whose function the revision no longer defines. Rules and owners added:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **W16-NS** (`O10_EDGE_ZERO`, `O10_SITE_ZERO`, `EDGE_PER_CALL`; route E `EDGE_ZERO`; guarded) | **Premise.** D1.7 refuses every support-targeted primitive on both routes: `family_clauses`' exhaustive match over every case's primitive loads returns `refuse(C::Loads, F::LoadTarget)` on the support arm, so an admitted request has none. Every `imposed_motion` site behind a support target is unreached: `validate`'s exact-branch loop (it iterates only support targets) and its refusal text; `validate_pressure_free` after its let-else (eight named refusals' texts; `motion` is set only there, so the motion-only nonlinear push, its id, message, refs and row, is unreached, and D1.6 also refuses nonlinear supports); `case_prescribed` after its let-else (its map stays empty, `any` false, nothing is pushed); and the case solve's early return, whose `load_case.id` clone follows a blocking diagnostic pushed after `before`. The edges from those branches are zero: `validate` → `refuse`, `case_prescribed` → `refuse`, `parse_dof`, `dof_index`, and `validate_pressure_free` → `is_rigid`, `restrains`, `expected_dimension`, `parse_dof`, `dof_index`, `diag`, `exact_source_identity`, `unit_by_symbol`; and by-name edges reached only there (a binding `support`, `support_id`, `Iterator::find`, `Iterator::sum`, `HashMap::get`) to unrelated functions of those names. `validate_pressure_free` → `refuse` is bounded per call by cases·l: on D1 only the node/element refusal calls it, at most once per primitive load. Route E zeroes `validate` → `validate_pressure_free`: route E is the exact branch (EXACT), and `validate` takes the pressure-free branch only when `is_exact` is false. **Guard:** D1.7's loop header and support refusal, `is_support_target`'s body, `validate`'s filtered loop, single `refuse(` and else branch, `validate_pressure_free`'s let-else with its one pre-`continue` refusal and no earlier `refuse(` or `diag(`, `motion = true;` once after the let-else, the nonlinear condition, `case_prescribed`'s let-else, single `refuse(`, single `.insert(` and `any = true;` after the let-else, the case solve's `before`/`case_prescribed`/`has_blocking` statement, one call of each entry in lib.rs and twelve `refuse(` in the module's production text, read at the snapshot; route E's file reads `validate`'s two branches. `tests/test_w16_imposed_motion.py` mutates each premise; KD5T2's body pin stops a changed case-solve return first. |
| **W16-ID** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`) | `refuse`'s refs clone the load and case ids (IN128). Its id is `diagnostic:imposed-displacement:` + `exact_source_identity` over the case id, the load id and the code: each part `{len}:{part}`, so 2 · (4 + 128) + (3 + 48) = 315 B, the code bounded by the module's longest code constant (`EXACT_IMPOSED_DISPLACEMENT_PRIMITIVE_UNSUPPORTED`, 48 B, read at the snapshot; the reachable call passes the 35 B `IMPOSED_DISPLACEMENT_TARGET_INVALID`). The node/element refusal's message names the load and case ids (IN128). Its `refuse` call passes `None`, so `refs.extend(support.map(str::to_string))` adds nothing on D1. The zeroed sites keep their expressions' classes, since the chain classifies a site before its zero applies: the ids IN128, a DOF token 2 B, and the nonlinear push's id over one IN128 id and its code. |
| **W16-NI** (`w16_owners_model.py`, positive owner, `ordinary_caps.py` CasePrefix) | `case_prescribed` collects `node_index`, a `HashMap<&str, usize>` over the model's n nodes from an exact-size iterator (one table, no growth), before its loop, in every case solved without a load state, on both routes; it lives to the function's return, one case at a time. Charged as one `(&str,usize)` table of n entries per case beside the case's own node index. Its `prescribed` `BTreeMap` allocates nothing on D1 (`BTreeMap::new` does not allocate; it is inserted into only after the let-else). |
| **W16-VF** (`w16_owners_model.py`, positive owner, BuildScratch) | `validate` collects its findings in `found`, a push-grown `Vec<Diagnostic>`, and moves them into the run's diagnostics (`diagnostics.extend(found)`). On D1 the node/element refusal pushes at most one per primitive load (route L), so `found` is at most pushcap(c·l) slots with its previous backing; the diagnostics' strings are TEXT (W16-ID) and their count is in D. Charged on both routes, an over-bound on route E, as W7-QF is. `validate_pressure_free`'s per-case `prescribed` `HashMap` and the DOF name's `to_ascii_uppercase()` are owned only in the support-target branch. |

**Guard of the owners.** `w16_owners_model` binds `imposed_motion.rs`'s sha256 and the owning statements (whitespace-normalized) with the two calls in lib.rs; a snapshot without the module adds nothing, and a module without its pin, or a lib.rs call without the module, stops the run.

The runs at the worktree state and at `6d4e0c1506` complete on all four points with exit 0, no unmapped header or unclassified argument and both o10 checks at zero; each of the five trees is coefficient-identical between them. D moves on every point: L(3,0) 43,525 → 43,781 (+384 node/element refusals, −128 for the deleted pre-parse refusal), L(2,1) 39,240 → 39,368, L(1,2) 35,235 (unchanged: +128, −128) and E 76,482 → 76,354 (−128). The merged D is 76,354 and D_env 28,902 → 28,774. Text(diag_env) rises 270,642,448 → 274,404,624 B (+3,762,176): route L(3,0)'s envelope gains the node/element refusals and is now the maximum over routes, with E's 228,109,150 B. Text(row) stays 11,474 B; P_FINAL (78 rows per member, 2,979) and P_FINAL_EXACT (114, 4,131) are unchanged. ### 8.24 Unit factor-sum gate actual-source D3

The pressure-free constant-effort gate's unit factor-sum rule (option C, Agent 0, 2026-10-25; [pressure-contract.md](../../DEL-04-07/Design/pressure-contract.md), "Constant-effort combinations: the unit factor-sum rule") re-prices at `0f0f28106e`, the commit that carries the pins below. It merges main `b7da9ff18a` (whose priced source is `1ed3a4d7fb`'s), so it is priced on W-16 slice a's registration (§8.23). The comparison basis is main's registered tree: main's committed tool prices `b7da9ff18a` with exit 0, and `generate --check` of that tree with §8.23's basis line reproduces §8.23's registered block with an empty diff. Against the basis the priced source moves in four files:
- PP `preview_physics.rs`: `gate_reason` replaces its two binary64 folds (`sum`, `abs_sum`) with one call of `open_pipe_stress_load_case_algebra::factor_sum_within_unit_tolerance(combination.terms.iter().map(|t| t.factor))`. An `Err` withholds under the same code. The test module adds one test;
- LA `factor_sum.rs` (new): the helper, `accumulate`, and `FactorSumError` with its `Display`;
- RE `retained_precision.rs` and `semantic_contract.rs`: the preview tables' digests (constants), after the tables restate the gate's condition.

No FK file changes, and no owner, loop or argument class is added.

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **UF-EX** (zero, by premise; no rule) | The helper's working set is three `ExactAccumulator`s: the sum and its two bound probes, `clone`d. Each is two `[u64; 68]` magnitudes (1,088 B), held on the stack. `new`, `add`, `add_shifted`, `add_word`, `compare` and `signum` (frame-kernel `exact_sum.rs`) work in place on those arrays and allocate nothing. The factor loop iterates the borrowed terms and allocates nothing. So no heap owner is added, and the run finds no new loop header or argument. The stack frame grows by about 3.3 KB in `gate_reason`'s callee; the AC-004 stack-headroom witnesses are separate from this registration. |
| **UF-TX** (positive, existing TEXT inventory) | `FactorSumError`'s `Display` writes are reached through the call graph's by-name `fmt` edge. They are priced at their two sites: 53 B and 37 B, requested 106 + 74 = 180 B in TAV_W and TAV_X. On the product path the gate maps any `Err` to a withheld gate (`unwrap_or(false)`) and never formats it. The charge is a conservative overcount, kept rather than zeroed. |
| **UF-ST** (positive, statics) | Both preview tables' `CONSTANT_EFFORT_COMBINATION_REQUIRES_SOLVE` condition grows by 111 B, and the resident table statics carry it three times, so STATICS rises by +333 B (529,644 → 529,977). |

**Pins and carried rules.** Every changed span was read before re-pinning. Full-file pins: preview_physics.rs (`5b1dc6f0…` → `878a16b8…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py` and `w1_reductions_model.py`; RE retained_precision.rs (`46f4163f…` → `2bdce1a1…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py` and `w1_member_load_model.py`; RE semantic_contract.rs (`c877df1d…` → `d7b5d38e…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py`. Each file's diff from main is exactly this change's gate, its test and the two table digests. No key is re-keyed. The line map prunes the same 37 rules as main's run, entry for entry. The edge rule naming `gate_reason`'s former `sum` call is now unused and stays.

The run at `0f0f28106e` completes on all four points with exit 0, no unmapped header or unclassified argument and both o10 checks at zero. `compare` against main's tree gives the same structure, `a_le_b` false and `a_ge_b` true: 1,578 coefficients are equal and 8 rise, none falls. The 8 are STATICS and `success__STATICS` +333 B; TAV_W (5,064,132,586 → 5,064,132,766 B), TAV_X (5,516,447,194 → 5,516,447,374 B) and their `success__` forms +180 B; and the text atoms TAV_text_requested (7,397,877,102 → 7,397,877,282 B) and TAV_text_moving (7,400,477,802 → 7,400,477,982 B) +180 B. ### 8.25 Nested-record closure actual-source D3

The pressure-free input closure's nested records ([pressure-contract.md](../../DEL-04-07/Design/pressure-contract.md), "Pressure-free input closure") re-price at `c9440263cf`, the commit that carries the pins below. It merges main `e2f9677175` (the unit factor-sum gate's registration, §8.24), so it is priced on §8.24. The comparison basis is main's registered tree: main's committed tool prices `e2f9677175` with exit 0, and `generate --check` of that tree with §8.24's basis line reproduces §8.24's registered block with an empty diff. Against the basis the priced source moves in two files:
- PP `lib.rs` closes the nested wire records: `deny_unknown_fields`, declared-and-ignored zero-size `IgnoredAny` members (model document 0.7.0's) and per-field closed quantities and vectors. The records are the node, the pipe segment with its section, the section, the support with its stiffness, hanger and nonlinear inputs, the component with its geometry, modifiers and mechanics interface, and the material's temperature point. The material record keeps main's flattened wire, so its outcomes are main's apart from an unknown key: its other keys are collected as `IgnoredAny` and a closing wrapper (`ClosedMaterialRecord`) refuses any but the four annotations. `MaterialInput` and its temperature point read their quantities through the closed deserializers, and the pipe section (`PipeSectionInput`) is closed in place. Seven test literals name the new members. The diff adds no allocation, format, `.min(`/`.max(` site or call on the priced path. Every snapshot-keyed line after the records moves +106 on main's with unchanged text.
- PP `model_fields.rs` adds the walk of the nested records, `nested_records`. It checks each record's keys in `check_nested` and forms a finding's name and path only there, through a borrowed frame (`At`, `finding_name`, `finding_path`). It also adds the closed mirrors (quantity, vector, vector quantity) and the objective connector's and the material's key lists. A scan keeps at most eight findings (`Findings::keep_finding`, which forms a finding only while it is kept and counts the rest), and both refusals are formed by `listed_refusal`. A pass run first (`non_object_records`) refuses, with `MODEL_RECORD_NOT_OBJECT` and its path, a model record or request material that is not an object, whose keys the scan cannot name.

`self_weight.rs`, which is unpriced, moves +8 lines at its one snapshot key (1284 → 1292, unchanged text). Its two evidence-reader `PreviewPipe` literals name the declared members, and the evidence's mass inputs are read through the closed pipe section after removing the one declared extra dependency, `section_ref`. `component_basis`, the lineage reader of a component-weight record, reads its recorded `weight_input` and `center_of_gravity_input` as closed quantities. `component_basis` is reached only from `inspect_applied_self_weight`, whose downstream functions W15B-NS zeroes. The generator reads a component's `id`, `kind`, `node`, `provenance`, `geometry.rigid_pipe_ref`, `geometry.weight`, `geometry.center_of_gravity` and `mechanics_interface.solver_consumption`, each a typed member of the closed records (the weight a closed quantity, the centre of gravity a closed vector quantity), and none of the component's declared-and-ignored members.

**Pins and carried rules.** Every changed span was read before re-pinning:
- **Full-file pins.** `lib.rs` (`8b437bcc…`, §8.23's and unchanged by §8.24, → `06e98114…`) in `b7a_domain_model.py`, `w1_reductions_model.py` and `kd5_text_model.py`. For the E13/E14 re-audit, the diff adds or removes no `.min(`/`.max(` call, the observation lane stays deleted and E13's retired preimage is unchanged. `self_weight.rs` (`da644546…`, §8.23's, → `e543bf48…`) in `b7a_formation_model.py`.
- **Owners.** No owner a model prices changes: the domain, formation, reduction, owner-choice and first-error owners read their unchanged statements. The SD owners (`sd_owners_model.py`) are unchanged: the walk adds no heap owner. Its frames and field lists are stack values and `'static` slices, and the probes of the added records drop their boxed text one at a time (SD-PB).
- **Snapshot keys.** They move with unchanged text, `lib.rs` +106 on main's: `b7a_domain_model.py`'s audits, `b7a_owner_choices.py`'s helpers (14425/14426/14846/14847 → 14531/14532/14952/14953), `b7a_phase_model.py`'s `SOLVE` (4917 → 5023) and `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner` (2752 → 2858), `b7a_returning_errors.py`'s owners, sites and callers, and `b7a_scoped_text_model.py`'s four lines. Each was remapped from main's key through a line map of main's `lib.rs` onto the merged one. `self_weight.rs` moves 1284 → 1292. The tool's tests follow.
- **Emitter and line map.** The `_X` emitter, with `retained_w1` carried to `lib.rs:3858`, adds hash `8e2d83e5…` to `MODE_SOURCE`. Its only difference from §8.23's `59bffb88…` (at `lib.rs:3752`) is that key. The line map carries every rule (873 remaps, no unmapped key). Its 37 prunes and seven resolutions are main's, entry for entry. W15B-NS's guard reads its statements unchanged at the merged snapshot.
- **Decision tables.** `rule_ops.carry_decision_keys` carries every upper-case name the decisions bind as a decision table written at the basis. Four model namespaces were bound under such names: the owner choices' (`_OC`), the W1 reductions' (`_W1_MODEL`), the W-7 owners' (`_W7`) and the C8 member model's (`_C8_MODEL`). So their snapshot keys were carried as if written at the basis. The carried copies were never read: the decisions read each model before the carry. On main, the owner choices' `lib.rs:14426` was carried by offset to an unrelated line. At this merge, `lib.rs:14532` fell in a replaced hunk and the run stopped (exit 4). Two inspected snapshot states were bound the same way: the C8 model's (`C8_MEMBER_STATE`, whose `site` the carry moved to an unrelated line) and the W1 reductions' (`_W1_REDUCED`, a flag). The namespaces and states are now bound under lower-case names, or not kept. The carry rule itself is unchanged. Main's tool with only the namespace change prices `e2f9677175` to a byte-identical tree, and the head prices identically before and after the state renames. `test_no_module_namespace_is_a_decision_table` and `test_no_model_state_is_bound_as_a_decision_table` bind it. The second evaluates both routes' decisions. It refuses any upper-case binding that no driver in `lib/` reads but that still holds a line key `map_key` would carry, whatever form binds it, and its control finds C8's state bound through `dict(...)`.

Rules added, by label:

| Rule | Argument |
|---|---|
| **SD-NR (zero)** (`O10_SITE_ZERO`, `O10_EDGE_ZERO`, `O10_RECURSION_REVIEWED`; guard) | **Premise.** SD-NF's: the walk and the non-object pass run inside the value entry's first statement, so an admitted request has no finding. Each finding is kept through `keep_finding` by its closure: in `check`, in `check_nested` and in `non_object_records`, each at multiplicity 0. A finding's name and path are formed only in `check_nested`'s closure, so its two calls (`check_nested` → `finding_name`, `finding_path`) are zero. The methods' other calls are their own recursion, one frame per enclosing record and at most four frames, reviewed. Both refusals are formed only by `listed_refusal`, called only in the scan's two refusing returns (`unknown_field_refusal` → `listed_refusal` zero), and SD-EV's by-name edge to `LoadLedger::push` now leaves `keep_finding`, whose `Vec::push` is model_fields' only `.push(`. **Guard:** that push, the three closures and the two returns are each where stated. The non-object pass runs once and returns before the record fields are built. The walk is called once, last before the scan's final return. `.finding_name()` and `.finding_path()` occur only in the closure and in their own bodies. A change stops the run. |
| **SD-NR (loops)** (`O10_LOOPS_FIRST`, `O10_LOOPS_LAST`) | The walk's loops, each only in its function, are placed first: the base chain's broad `material` and `point in` rules would otherwise capture two of the exact headers. The model's nodes (n), pipe segments (m) and supports (g) are each visited once (D1.9). The sections are 0 (S-4), and so are their properties. The components are 0 (P2). The model's and the request's material collections are 2, with at most 4 materials each (mats = 8), and one material's temperature points are at most pts. One record's static quantity list is at most its longest, 13, counted at the snapshot. The key loop (`for key in record.keys()`) now bounds both `check` and `check_nested` at the largest closed record's declared members: the component geometry's 31, counted from the structs. The model root's 23 bound it before. The run stops if another record is larger, or the material's key list (13) outgrows it. The non-object pass visits its two owners (2), the model's eight record collections (read at the snapshot; a changed list stops the run) and one collection's records, at most n on D1. |
| **SD-NR (texts)** (`O10_ID_AUDIT`, `LEX_SITE_SIZE`) | The frame's own sites, at multiplicity 0, bounded regardless. A record's name is `{kind} `{id}`` or `{kind} {index}`. The kind is one of the walk's eight static kinds, at most 17 B ("temperature point"); the id is IN128. A member adds `the {key} of `, its key a static member name or a section property's name (IN128). An entry adds `{kind} `{id}` of `. A path is `{collection}[{index}]` (collection ≤ 19 B) plus `.{key}` or `.temperature_points[{index}]` per frame. At most three members are below a record (a hanger's stiffness value), or one entry and one member (a temperature point's quantity). So a name is at most 556 B and a path 428 B. The refusal text's record and path bounds (SD-ID) take these maxima. A non-object finding is `{path} is {kind}`: the path is `model.` (≤ 6 B), a static collection (≤ 13 B) and a usize index, at most 41 B, and the kind is static (≤ 30 B). The joined list is bounded by eight of the longer finding, and `listed_refusal`'s code (≤ 23 B) and remedy (≤ 154 B) are static. The frame names records at its own sites, not through `named`, so `named`'s kind class (14 B) and its 387 chain calls on the root records are unchanged. **Guard:** the kinds, collections and entry key are read at the snapshot; a change stops the run. |

The runs at `c9440263cf` and at `4a5544d2bc` (the merge, with this commit's tool) complete on all four points with exit 0, no unmapped header or unclassified argument and both o10 checks at zero. The tree is coefficient-identical to main's (`identical_but_basis`; 1,586 coefficients equal, none higher or lower), and so is each point's (L(3,0) 1,584, L(2,1) and L(1,2) 1,588, E 1,556). D (L(3,0) 43,781, L(2,1) 39,368, L(1,2) 35,235, E 76,354), D_env (28,774), each point's E+R, TAV_W and TAV_X (E 5,064,132,766 B and 5,516,447,374 B) and the merged check values (sparse 8,705,610,764 B, dense 8,764,742,108 B) are main's. P_FINAL (78 rows per member) and P_FINAL_EXACT (114) are unchanged. The points' self-recursive lists differ from main's only by SD-NR's reviewed `finding_name` and `finding_path` and by `retained_basis`' moved line (916 → 918).

Before this merge, `f6d983963d` (on main `3027cf2f35`) measured coefficient-identical to §8.22's tree, and `3ee39ea354` (on main `aed4de9fbd`) to §8.21's. ### 8.26 Model document 0.8.0 and a reducer's eccentric offset actual-source D3 (W-15 slice c)

### 8.27 Exact-route load combinations and equivalent-static generation actual-source D3 (W-16 slices b and c)

W-16 slice b (DEL-05-02 Design [exact-combinations.md](../../../PKG-05/DEL-05-02/Design/exact-combinations.md) §9) and slice c (DEL-05-01 Design [exact-equivalent-static.md](../../../PKG-05/DEL-05-01/Design/exact-equivalent-static.md) §6) re-price together at `3493838a8b`, the commit that carries the pins and rules below. The comparison basis is main's registered tree: main's committed tool prices `187b67bb6b` with exit 0, and `generate --check` of that tree with §8.26's shear-model basis line reproduces the registered block (1,695 lines, an empty diff). Neither design changes P_FINAL or P_FINAL_EXACT, and neither moves. Against the basis the priced source moves in these files:
- PP `lib.rs`: `mod exact_combinations;`; in `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner`, the connector evidence is built once before the combinations (it was built inside the envelope's evidence closure; the same `is_exact` condition and allocation, an empty record list under D1's P3), `exact_combinations::append` runs under `is_exact(&model) && !model.combinations.is_empty()`, its records enter the evidence as `exact_combinations`, and `state_operand_standing` runs on that member; `solve_prepared_load_case` adds the `equivalent_static` record under `load_case.equivalent_static.is_some()`; `append_equivalent_static_generated_loads` gains, after its let-else return, the exact-route mass (`generation_mass_per_length`), the replaced-span skips (`wind_span_replaced`), the Timoshenko partial-span refusal and `refuse_generated_id_collisions`; `is_generated_equivalent_static_load` and `exact_equivalent_static_record` are new; `algebra_dimension` reads `Pa` and `m` (an allocation-free match; the pressure-free route passes no row in either unit to this match). The diff adds no `.min(`/`.max(` call. The snapshot keys move with unchanged text: +1 after line 151, +28 after 3436, +33 after 7010, +193 after 10814 and +198 after 14539 (main's numbering).
- PP `exact_combinations.rs` (new): the combination evaluator, reached only through `append` and `state_operand_standing`.
- PP `certified_loads.rs`: `intensity`'s two generated-id prefix tests call `is_generated_equivalent_static_load` inside its existing `if let Some(generation) = &case.equivalent_static` arm, and `mass` takes the route; PP `self_weight.rs`: `exact_generation_mass_per_length`; PP `source_receipt/composite.rs`: `physical_source_built` re-forms the record under `case.equivalent_static.is_some()`; PP `pressure_runtime.rs`: `validate_profile` refuses a generating case on a load/reference-state document, inside its existing `case.equivalent_static.is_some()` arm; PP `exact_admission.rs`: the identity text (S1 and S2, 32 B shorter) and the load/reference-state limitation (147 B longer), and the two new refusal codes and texts.
- RE `exact_combination_evidence.rs` (new) and `lib.rs`'s `mod`; `physics_evidence.rs`: `validate_pressure_evidence` dispatches on `exact_combination_evidence::present` and its former body is `validate_direct_pressure_evidence`; `validate_physics_evidence_in` and `physics_source.rs`'s `validate_transport_metadata_in` check an `equivalent_static` record when one is present; `reader_verdict.rs`'s pressure arm adds the combination model checks (not reached from the retained route); `retained_precision.rs` and `semantic_contract.rs`: table hashes.

The source-block reader's support decision now mirrors the solver's: a constant-effort support is refused, and a spring is family `spring` exactly or a variable spring hanger as the solver's `support_hanger_type` reads it. The branch changes no file under `tools/retained_pricing/`. Against the basis the priced source moves in one file, RE `source_blocks.rs`:
- line 25, `COMPONENTS`, and line 1120, `fn supports(`: `pub(crate)`, for the test module below;

The span keeps its seven lines, so no line before 1491 moves, and line 1217 is now the `);` that closes the call instead of the second `matches!`;
- lines 1491–1547 (new, before the first `#[cfg(test)]`, now at line 1548): doc comments and four functions, `solver_spring_support` (1505–1511), `solver_support_hanger_type` (1516–1522), `solver_constant_effort_support` (1526–1531) and `solver_built_support` (1540–1546). Test-only files: RE `arc_evidence.rs` appends the declaration `#[cfg(test)] #[path = "source_blocks_spring_tests.rs"] mod source_blocks_spring_tests;` after its last line, `source_blocks_spring_tests.rs` is new, and PP `source_receipt/tests.rs` adds one test. The Python verifier `tests/verifiers/source_blocks.py` is not priced. PP `lib.rs` and every other priced source file are main's.

| Item | Argument |
|---|---|
| **SB-MR** (no owner, loop or TEXT site; no rule) | Each function indexes a borrowed `&Value`, and serde_json's `Index` returns the member or the static `Null`. `as_str` borrows. `str::trim` returns a subslice, scanning one string in library code without allocating. `filter`, `or_else`, `==` and `matches!` compare borrowed `&str`.
