### 8.30 The shared-terminal junction refusal, the nonlinear dense memory guard, the hanger-load warning and partial-span wind on Timoshenko members actual-source D3 (three corrections and W-16t)

One combined registration covers branch `claude/piping-830-corrections-20261028`. It carries three corrections (DEL-04-07 Design [pressure-contract.md](../../DEL-04-07/Design/pressure-contract.md) "Shared-terminal junctions" and [memory-budget-decision.md](../../DEL-04-07/Design/memory-budget-decision.md) "Nonlinear dense loop guard", and the hanger-load warning) with Codex's two numerical-review fixes. It also merges #1678's W-16t (DEL-05-01 Design [exact-equivalent-static.md](../../../PKG-05/DEL-05-01/Design/exact-equivalent-static.md) "Partial-span Timoshenko (§3)").

The combination re-prices at `03436d1ff0`, the commit that carries the pins and rules below. It merges main `47774b4727`, which carries §8.29's registration, so it is priced on §8.29.

**Comparison basis.** The basis is main's registered tree. Main's committed tool prices `47774b4727` with exit 0, and `generate --check` of that tree with §8.29's basis line reproduces the registered block (1,695 lines, an empty diff).

**P_FINAL.** No design changes P_FINAL or P_FINAL_EXACT, and neither moves (78 and 114 rows per member).

Against the basis the priced source moves in these files:
- PP `lib.rs`:
  - `run_linear_static_preview_observed_inner` refuses an invocation once, under `if !built.nonlinear_supports.is_empty()`, when NI's `dense_loop_memory_guard` finds the loop's estimated leading dense storage above its limit. The refusal is `NONLINEAR_DENSE_MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`, with a blocked envelope.
  - `append_equivalent_static_generated_loads` loses the Timoshenko partial-span refusal and its two locals.
- PP `exact_admission.rs`: the shared-terminal junction code and its text (in Codex's wording) replace W-16 slice c's Timoshenko partial-span code and text.
- PP `pressure_runtime.rs`: `validate_profile` calls the new `refuse_compensated_shared_terminals` once per exact case after its region loop. That function maps the case's region terminals by node in a `BTreeMap` of two `Vec`s per node, and refuses a node with two or more ending regions and a compensated terminal.
- PP `validation.rs`: after the variable spring hanger metadata passes validation, `validate_variable_spring_hanger` warns once for each entered nonzero installed, cold or hot load that it is not applied as preload (`SPRING_HANGER_LOAD_NOT_APPLIED_AS_PRELOAD`).
- NI `lib.rs`:
  - `DenseLoopMemoryRefusal`, `dense_loop_memory_estimate` (a `HashSet` of the in-range restrained DOFs) and `dense_loop_memory_guard`;
  - the error's `DenseMemoryLimitExceeded` arm and its Display;
  - the active-set loop entry runs the guard before `validate_input`.
- SP `lib.rs`: `equivalent_global_nodal_loads_with_spans_formed` bounds a Timoshenko member's partial-span load (`timoshenko_partial_bounds`, with its expansion vectors, accumulators and bounds `Vec`) in place of `CannotBound`.
- FK `structural/formation_check.rs`: `uniform_straight_balls` re-forms that load through `timoshenko_partial_shear_terms`, with a stack `planes` array of eight balls on every call (no heap), and the partial-span refusal is gone.

No reader, schema, contract table, identity text or ledger layout changes, and no reviewed input moves.

The tail is the static constant `SHARED_TERMINAL_JUNCTION_TEXT`, at its literal length read at the snapshot. |
| **C30-DG** (`O10_EDGE_ZERO`, `O10_SITE_ZERO`, `O10_ARGS_FIRST`; guard) | **Premise.** D1.6 admits no nonlinear support. The case solve calls the guard only under `if !built.nonlinear_supports.is_empty()`, so the edge to `dense_loop_memory_guard` is zero, with its restraint `HashSet`, and so are the refusal diagnostic's format, row and refs clone. The two u128 byte counts are classed at 39 B. **Guard:** the one guarded call, the one `dense_loop_memory_guard(` in `lib.rs`, the code within that block, and the Display's two arguments. |

PP's one `UniformLoadSpan::new` is in `straight_global_uniform_load`, and the primitive crate's one extent-bearing constructor call is the wind exposure's `Some(extent)` arm. FK's `timoshenko_partial_shear_terms` returns `None` before any operation for an Euler–Bernoulli frame or a full span, and its `planes` array is a fixed stack array, not a heap owner. **Guard:** the one call behind `is_timoshenko() && uniform_loads.iter().any(partial)`, the partial predicate, PP's one span constructor, and the primitive crate's one extent-bearing call. |

### 8.31 Nonlinear pressure-free combination operand standing actual-source D3 (slice 1a, RT-A5)

This turn prices branch `claude/piping-op1a-operand-standing-20261028`, source `0fdda050b0`, which merges main `9ef6c4c469`. **Comparison basis.** Main's committed tool prices `9ef6c4c469` with exit 0. With §8.30's registered basis line, `generate --check` reproduces its 1,695-line registered block exactly. A second replay retains the new OP1A rules in a scratch copy of main's complete tool cohort, with main's pins and snapshot keys; the OP1A branch adds nothing when its producer helper is absent. This checks the preceding registration without selectively mixing historical source models.

**P_FINAL.** Neither P_FINAL nor P_FINAL_EXACT moves: 78 and 114 rows per member.

Against main the priced source changes in these files (every changed span was read before re-pinning):
- PP `lib.rs`: test-only seed instrumentation, an empty-on-D1 `preview_operands` vector beside the guarded combination append, and its publication call.
- PP `preview_physics.rs`: nonlinear-only operand records, support-state comparison and notices; publication returns immediately on empty records.
- PP `exact_combinations.rs`: two helper visibilities, and the standing lookup additionally requires `basis_ref.ref_type == "load_case"`. This borrows another JSON key, with no new allocation or owner. Both exact and preview callers' record bodies are unreached on D1.
- PP `op1a_seed_tests.rs`: entirely `#[cfg(test)]`; no production owner.
- RE `lib.rs`: the new reader module declaration. RE `preview_physics_evidence.rs`: the optional third evidence key and one reader call. RE `reader_verdict.rs`: the model-bound check, outside the retained precommit roots. RE `preview_combination_operands.rs`: the reader below.
- The two ordinary result schemas add the optional record. Neither is a resident reader cache or one of the twelve registered reviewed inputs. No layout or reviewed input moves. `retained_memory.rs` is unchanged.

**Route and lifetime review.** R1's original preview-physics-1 wording is historical. `retained_precision::ROUTE_L.base_id` is now preview-physics-2 (the already guarded U4B pair), `project` writes that base id, and G7 calls `semantic_contract::for_source` on the projection. Its PREVIEW_PHYSICS_ID arm therefore reaches the new reader on L. Route E's base is pressure-2 and does not take that arm. D1.6 excludes nonlinear supports, so the producer returns an empty record vector and emits neither notice. The reader still scans rows for a nonlinear state, collects admitted gate ids, checks presence and scans diagnostics; its solved-nonlinear mechanics scan is short-circuited. The record loop, operand evaluation order, quality lookup/recursive bit comparison, row and source-reference searches, duplicate-state and slip-sense searches, notice construction and notice comparison have zero outer multiplicity. The model-bound `reader_verdict` entry is not a retained precommit root.

The law tests' native phase pins move by the E + R increments above.

Coefficients and registration move, so Agent 0's instruction applies: "if coefficients move, re-register and re-run AC-004 with my exclusive grant". The six AC-004 stack-headroom witnesses (`witness_headroom_w1_at_one_mebibyte`, `witness_w2_deep_milestone_publishes` and `witness_w_c2_publishes`, each sparse and dense, one process each) passed **6/6 at `84824b20f0`**, rustc 1.97.1, on the granted exclusive host lane at `--test-threads=1`, using `python3 tools/release/check_release_readiness.py --profile stack-witnesses --execute`. These existing results are reused on the coefficient-identical source confirmation; the witnesses are not re-run in this registration turn. **This is test evidence, not an acceptance.** Independent review of this registration is arranged by the manager.

## 9. Memory-profile gates and registration

### Gate bounds (registration)

G-C (`retained_memory.rs phase_caps_on`) must admit what D1 produces and stay within what the profile prices. Route L's P_final and I95's pre-D-5 evidence census are below what the cap-maximal exact input publishes (`witness_tests::f1_g_c_admits_...`), so route E needs its own bounds.
- **Route E.** The result rows and their capacity are C_eq·P_final at route E's own P_final = 7n + 114m + 8g + 3 (12,393 and 16,384 at the caps; U4B-RP, U6-RP, U6-RB-V, W7-RP). The five contract-evidence bounds are C times the D-5 census at (n, m, g): arrays 14 + 78m + 19n, objects 4 + 27m, entries 29 + 127m, string bytes 6080 + 12464m + 304n, key bytes 495 + 1348m (U6-RL). At the caps these are 9,354 array slots; 2,604 objects and 12,274 entries after the node cut, so objects + ⌊entries / 5⌋ = 5,058 nodes; 1,243,968 string bytes; and 130,893 key bytes. They equal route E's chain terms ("Final aggregation" and "Preview tree final copy (envelope evidence)": 5,058·Node(String,Value) + 9,354·s(Value) + 1,374,861 B).
- **Row text.** The rows' text bound stays route L's 2·C_eq·P_final·Text(row) on both routes: Text(row) bounds one row's text, and route E's rows stay below route L's text rows at every capacity, since 7n + 114m + 8g + 3 ≤ 2·(7n + 78m + 8g + 3) for all n, m, g ≥ 0 (at the caps 12,393 against 17,874; route L's P_final is 7n + 78m + 8g + 3 by U4B-RL and W7-RL).
- **Route L.** Its result rows and their capacity are C_eq·P_final at P_final = 7n + 78m + 8g + 3 (8,937 and 16,384 at the caps; U4B-RL, W7-RL); its other bounds are unchanged.

**O4-EV.** A Timoshenko member's `pipe_sections` entry states `beam_theory` {model, shear_factor, shear_area_m2}: two number entries (25 key bytes) per member beyond the Euler-Bernoulli census entry. G-C's bounds stay the census (product gate, unchanged), so a cap-maximal Timoshenko input reaches them at a smaller m and is refused there; G-C's 16,384-value limit binds first in either model. The profile charges the two entries positively beside the census owner (§8.9).

Two limits keep these bounds fail-closed rather than reachable at the census maximum. The census counts each case, not the evidence wrapper (one object, its three keys `connector`, `exact_cases` and `pressure`, 28 B, and the cases' array slots), and the entries bound is cut to the priced nodes, so an owner at exactly C times the census maximum would be refused. G-C's borrowed census also stops at 16,384 values (`VALUE_LIMIT`), below the route-E bounds' 21,628 array slots and entries, and G-C refuses an incomplete census. Neither is reached by a lawful input: a source-selected run never consults G-C (`Coexistence`), and the retained-source method's per-case work budget keeps selected inputs to a few members (a three-case line of four members already exhausts it). The witness composes a near-cap owner from a selected run's own tied entries: at m = 23 and n = 32 (C = 3) it is within route E's bounds with a complete census, and at m = 24 the census reaches its value limit.

### Registration and qualification

- **Generated block.** `generate` replaces only the GENERATED PROFILE block and keeps `REGISTERED_PROFILES.threshold_bytes`; it does not select or register M. `generate --check` confirms that a committed block is the generator's output for a tree. The law tests pin the in-build phase record, so the merged tree's E + R is checked against the in-build value in all 16 phase/mode identities (14 before §8.14).
- **Registered profile.** `REGISTERED_PROFILES` binds the exact build identity (rustc 1.97.1, aarch64-apple-darwin, debug, opt-level 0, debug assertions, unwind, no RUSTFLAGS, `open_pipe_stress_product_physics@0.2.0`), the reviewed-input record, the reader layouts (`READER_LAYOUTS`) and `threshold_bytes` = M. The reviewed inputs are the PP lock, the reader statics in their preserved order, the resident pressure-source table, the nonresident pressure result schema, the resident ordinary pressure table and the resident PF Timoshenko table (thirteen inputs); duplicate parsed caches remain priced. A build whose identity, inputs or layouts differ is Stale. The current reviewed source is the combined PF Timoshenko, transverse-shear factor and canonical-decimal temperature identity stack (`84824b20f0`, confirmed at `21fe23fed1`, §8.32; 12 coefficients higher than the preceding registration). The current registration is §8.32's, with thirteen reviewed inputs. Slice 1a's nonlinear pressure-free operand standing (`0fdda050b0`, §8.31), coefficient-identical to its carried registration, preceded it. That carried registration was §8.30's three corrections and W-16t's partial-span wind on Timoshenko members, on the tree priced at `03436d1ff0` (§8.30; 6 coefficients higher). W-16 stage 2's imposed displacement with nonlinear supports and on spring bases, on the tree priced at `0bf192cbf8` (§8.29; 16 coefficients higher), preceded it. W-16 slices b and c's exact-route load combinations and equivalent-static generation, on the tree priced at `3493838a8b` (§8.27; 6 coefficients higher and 6 lower), preceded it. The source-block reader's support decision mirror (#1686, §8.28) prices coefficient-identical to it at `787c0535aa`, so that block stands. Model document 0.8.0's with its shear-model member, on the tree priced at `642e9737e4` (§8.26, "The shear-model member"; 92 coefficients higher), preceded it. Model document 0.8.0's with a reducer's eccentric offset (W-15 slice c), on the tree priced at `d5b71e2dda` (§8.26), preceded it; that was coefficient-identical to the nested-record closure's, on the tree priced at `c9440263cf` (§8.25), which preceded it; that was coefficient-identical to the unit factor-sum gate's, on the tree priced at `0f0f28106e` (§8.24), which preceded it. W-16 slice a's imposed displacement on the pressure-free route, on the tree priced at `6d4e0c1506` (§8.23), preceded it. W-15 slice b's component weight and centre of gravity as nodal weight loads, on the tree priced at `600a04436d` (§8.22), preceded it; that was coefficient-identical to W-15's rigid-component stiffness scaling and reference-section identification, on the tree priced at `c7318c32f9` (§8.21), which preceded it. W-7's member end actions in global coordinates, on the tree priced at `8afaa282b3` (§8.20), preceded it. The silent-drop guard's, on the tree priced at `43856d7964` (§8.19), preceded it. The absolute node-proximity tolerance's, on the tree priced at `2b72ac3af7` (§8.18), coefficient-identical to its first pricing at `f985fe297a`, preceded it. Model document 0.7.0's, on the tree priced at `7222d954a8` (§8.17), preceded it; that was coefficient-identical to the silent-zero guard's on top of W-8, on the tree priced at the merge of `1cb6b275f6` with main `f0b6ae40a0` (§8.15), and W-8's at `34e96d6b17` (§8.16) preceded that.
- **Law tests.** The law tests (`cargo test --lib profile_` in `core/product_physics`) pin the in-build phase record, the text atoms (for example D_env 28,799 and Text(diag_env) 269,494,417 at the T4-U4b/U5b registration, §8.8, unchanged at O4's, §8.9), M and ⌊0.9 M⌋, R, and the digests. Their `I65_G5_ATOM` lines feed `eval`.
- **Atoms.** Native atoms come from the same implementation's in-build collector. Evaluating route L's unmerged combination shapes additionally needs the native `Node(String,BTreeMap)` atom (648 B in RF's same-build layout evidence). No illustrative layout fills a missing atom.
- **Qualification.** A complete `run` is a pricing-completeness result. Registration additionally requires the native atoms, all 80 budget evaluations, the reviewed inputs and layouts, and independent source and pricing scrutiny; a source proof in this text alone confers no budget pass.

## 10. Reproducing a registered revision

The committed tool's source pins hold one current value each, so the committed tool reproduces only the revision those pins bind (now §8.32's combined source, `84824b20f0`, confirmed at `21fe23fed1`); at an earlier revision a pin stops it (exit 4). Historical replays must use their complete historical tool cohort and source basis, recovered through Git, rather than selectively restoring pins in today's tool. The current `ordinary_caps.py` and `g4_caps.py` require W1 reduction and stage-2 member models whose premises do not hold on the older trees below.

The following are historical replay results, not procedures for the current tool.
